oṃ
ṛgvedasaṃhitā śrīmatsāyaṇācāryaviracitabhāṣyasametā
atha dvitīyaṃ maṇḍalam ||
śatarcināṃ prathamaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vyākhyātam |
atha gārtsamadaṃ dvitīyaṃ maṇḍalaṃ vyākhyāyate |
‘tvamagne dyubhiḥ ’ ityādidaṃ dvitīyaṃ maṇḍalam |
catvāro'nuvākāḥ |
teṣu prathame'nuvāke ekādaśa sūktāni |
tatra ‘tvamagne dyubhiḥ ’ iti ṣoḍaśarcaṃ prathamaṃ sūktam |
maṇḍaladraṣṭā gṛtsamada ṛṣiḥ |
sa ca pūrvam āṅgirasakule śunahotrasya putraḥ san yajñakāle'surairgṛhīta indreṇa mocitaḥ |
paścāt tadvacanenaiva bhṛgukule śunakaputro gṛtsamadanāmā abhūt |
tathā cānukramaṇikā—‘ya āṅgirasaḥ śaunahotro bhūtvā bhārgavaḥ śaunako'bhavat sa gṛtsamado dvitīyaṃ maṇḍalampaśyat ’ iti |
tathā tasyaiva śaunakasya vacanam ṛtyanu- kramaṇe—‘tvamagna iti gṛtsamadaḥ śaunako bhṛgutāṃ gataḥ |
śaunahotraḥ prakṛtyā tu ya āṅgirasa ucyate ’ iti |
tasmānmaṇḍaladraṣṭā śaunako gṛtsamada ṛṣiḥ |
jagatī chandaḥ |
maṇḍalādiparibhāṣayā āgneyam |
atrānukramaṇikā—‘tvamagne jāgataṃ tu ’ iti ||
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayorjāgate chandasi idamādi- sūktadvayasya viniyogaḥ |
‘tvamagne dyubhiriti sūkte ’ ( āśva. śrau. 4. 13 ) iti sūtritatvāt ||
upākaraṇotsarjanayorapi2 ādyā viniyuktā ||
tvamagne dyubhistvamāśuśukṣaṇistvamaṅgirastvamaśmanaspari |
tvaṃ vanebhyastvamoṣadhībhyastvaṃ nṛṇāṃ nṛpate jāyase śuciḥ ||
1 ||
tvam | agne | dyubhiḥ | tvam | āśuśukṣaṇiḥ | tvam | aṅgiraḥ | tvam | aśmanaḥ | pari | tvam | vanebhyaḥ | tvam | oṣadhībhyaḥ | tvam | nṛṇām | nṛpate | jāyase | śuciḥ || 1 ||
he 'gne agraṇītvādiguṇaviśiṣṭa he 'nṛṇāṃ 'nṛpate |
nṛṇāṃ manuṣyāṇāṃ madhye ye narā yaṣṭāraḥ santi teṣāṃ viśeṣeṇa pālayitaḥ |
yadvā ubhābhyāṃ padābhyāṃ bahutvaṃ nityasvāmitvaṃ ca pratipāyate3 |
sarveṣāmapi narāṇāṃ nityapālakatyarthaḥ ||
nṛṇāmityasya nighātābhāvaśchāndasaḥ ||
tādṛśa he deva 'tvaṃ 'dyubhiḥ yāgadivasairanimittabhūtaiḥ 'jāyase yāgārthaṃ mathanena4 utpadyasa ityarthaḥ |
yadvā |
tādṛśairdivasaiḥ sahito jāyase |
tattadyāgadivaseṣu prasiddhaḥ5 bhavasītyarthaḥ |
tathā 'tvam 'āśuśukṣaṇiḥ jāyase ā sarvato
1. ṇa-ṇa-na-va- nu | 2. ma-upākarmotsarjanayorāpi | 3. ṇa-pratipadyate | 4. ṇa-ma- manthanena | 5. ka-jha-ma-pravṛddhaḥ |
dīpyamāno bhavasi ||
āṅugupapade 'śuca dīptau' ityetasya sanantasya chāndasamidaṃ rūpam |
yadvā |
āśu śīghraṃ śuca dīptaḥ san yataḥ saṃsevyate iti |
āśu śucaṃ śokaṃ sanoti dadāti śatrubhyo dāhādinā ityāśuśukṣaṇaḥ ||
tādṛśo bhavasi |
evaṃ sarvatra prativiśeṣaṇaṃ jāyasa iti yojyam |
he agne tvam adbhyaḥ vṛṣṭyudakebhyo jāyase vaiśvatarūpeṇa vāḍavarūpeṇa vā samudrodakebhyaḥ |
aśmanaḥ meghātpāṣāṇādvā pari jāyase |
'yo aśmanorantaragniṃ jajāna' ( ṛ. saṃ. 2. 12. 3 ) iti hi mantrāntaram |
vanebhyaḥ vṛkṣādisamūhebhyo dāvarūpeṇa |
tvam oṣadhībhyaḥ vaiśvānarūpeṇa' |
evaṃ mahānubhāvastvaṃ mathanenotpannaḥ san sarvātmako bhavasītyarthaḥ |
śuciḥ eva jāyase |
yadvā |
śuciḥ ādityarūpaḥ jāyasa ityarthaḥ |
atra 'tvamagne dyubhirahobhiḥ' ( niru. 6. 1 ) ityādi niruktamanusaṃdheyam |
tavā̭gne॒ hotra॒ṃ tarva̭ po॒tramṛ॒tviya॒ṃ tarva̭ ne॒ṣṭraṃ tvama॒gnida̭tā॒yata̭ḥ |
tarva̭ pra॒śāsta॒ṃ tvama̭dhva॒rīya̭si॒ brahmā̭ cā॒si gṛ॒hapa̭tiśca॒ no damḙ ||
2 ||
tarva | agne | hotram | tarva | potram | ṛtviyam | tarva | neṣṭram | tvam | agnida | ṛta'yataḥ | tarva | praśāstram | tvam | adhvarīyasi | brahmā | ca | asi | gṛha'patiḥ | ca | naḥ | dame ||2||
he agne hotraṃ hotṛḥ karma śastrādyādimakṣaṇaṃ yadasti tadapi tava eva svadarthamityarthaḥ |
tathā potraṃ potuḥ karma yadasti tadapi tava eva |
tathā ṛtviyaṃ prāptakālamātvijyaṃ tavaiva |
neṣṭraṃ neṣṭuḥ karma prasthitayājyādirūpaṃ tava |
he agne tvam agnida āgnīdhra tvamevāsi |
tathā ṛtāyataḥ yajñamicchataḥ tava eva praśāstaṃ yatpraśāstṛmantrāvaruṇasya karma tat |
he agne tvam adhvarīyasi adhvaraṃ yajñaṃ kāmayase |
adhvaryuṃbhavasītyarthaḥ |
tathādhvaryuśabdavyutpatteḥ |
'adhvaryurdhvarayurdhvaraṃ kāmayata iti vā' ( niru. 1.8 ) iti hi niruktam |
tathā brahmā brāhmaṇācchaṃsyapi asi |
tathā no dame asmadyajñagṛhe gṛhapatiśca asiti ||
tvama̭gne॒ indro̭ vṛṣa॒bhaḥ sa॒tāma̭si॒ tvaṃ vi॒ṣṇurṷru॒gāyo॒ nama̭syaḥ |
tvaṃ brahmā̭ ra॒yivi̭ddhrahmaṇaspate॒ tvaṃ vi̭dha॒rtaḥ sa̭case॒ pura̭ṃdhyā ||
3 ||
tvam | agne | indraḥ | vṛṣabhaḥ | satām | asi | tvam | viṣṇuḥ | uru'gāyaḥ | namasyaḥ | tvam | brahmā | rayi'vit | brahmaṇaḥ | pate | tvam | vidhartaḥ | sacase | puraṃdhyā || tvam | brahmā | rayi'vit | brahmaṇaḥ | pate | tvam | vidhartariti vi'dhartaḥ | sacase | puraṃdhyā || 3 ||
he agne tvaṃ yataḥ satāṃ vṛṣabhaḥ varṣitā asi ataḥ indraḥ asi |
yadvā |
satāṃ sādhūnām indro'si |
kāmānāṃ varṣitāsi |
tvaṃ viṣṇuḥ asi |
yataḥ urugāyaḥ bahubhirgīyamānaḥ namasyaḥ namaskāryaśca bhavasi |
he brahmaṇaspate parivṛḍhasya karmaṇo mantrasya vā pālayitaḥ tvaṃ yataḥ rayivit
1 ka-jña-1-ma-trītyādibhyo vaiśvānarūpeṇa |
2 ta-ma-va-ādityarūpeṇa |
3 ka-ṇa-1-na-va- brāhmaṇācchandasyapi |
ma. 2. a. 1, sū. 1 ] dvitīyo'ṣṭakaḥ 3
gavādinastasya vettā ataḥ brahmā asi|
he vidhartaḥ vividhakāraka vaiśvānararūpāme puraṃdhyā bahu- prakārayā buddhyā sacase saha vartase sarvadā buddhyā ratyasa ityarthaḥ|
tvama̭gne॒ rājā॒ varṷṇo dhṛtavra॒tastvaṃ mi॒tro bha̭vāsi॒ dasma̭ ī॒ḍya̭ḥ |
tvama̭rya॒mā sa॒tparti̭ryasya॒ saṃbhṷja॒ṃ tvama॒ṃśau vi̭da॒the de॒va bhā̭jayuḥ ||
4 ||
tvam | agne | rājā | varuṇaḥ | dhṛtavrataḥ | tvam | mitraḥ | bhavasi | dasmaḥ | īḍyaḥ | tvam | aryamā | satpatiḥ | yasya | sambhujam | tvam | aṃśaḥ | vidathe | deva | bhājayuḥ ||4||
he agne tvaṃ yataḥ dhṛtavrataḥ dhāritakarmāsi ataḥ varuṇaḥ rājā asi |
yataḥ dasmaḥ śatrūṇāmupakṣapayitā īḍyaḥ stutyaśca ataḥ mitraḥ etannāmako devo'si |
yataḥ satpatiḥ satāmabhimat- pradānena pālayitā ataḥ aryamā bhavasi |
dātṛttvaṃ ca aryamṇo liṅgam |
aryameti tamāhuryo dadātīti |
yasya aryamṇaḥ dānaṃ saṃbhujaṃ satatabhujaṃ vyāpakaṃ bhavati |
yadvā |
yasya dhanaṃ saṃbhujaṃ samyak bhogāya sādhu grahītṛṇām |
tathā tvam aṃśaḥ sūryamūryantarabhūta etannāmako devo'si |
tādṛśa he deva vidathe asmadīye yajñe bhājayuḥ phalānāṃ bhājayitā prāpayitāsi |
tvama̭gne॒ tvaṣṭā̭ vi॒dhate॒ suvī̭rya॒ṃ tva grā॒vo mi̭tra॒maha̭ḥ sa॒jātya̭m |
tvāśṷhe॒mā ra॒riṣe॒ svaśvya॒ṃ tvaṃ nṛ॒ṇāṃ śardho̭ asi॒ purṷvasuḥ ||
5 ||
tvam | agne | tvaṣṭā | vidhate | suvīryam | tve | grāvaḥ | mitramahaḥ | sajātyam | tvam | āśuhemā | rariṣe | svaśvyam | tvam | nṛṇām | śardhaḥ | asi | puruvasuḥ ||5||
he agne tvaṣṭā phalasya sādhu saṃpādayitā tvaṃ vidhate paricarate janāya suvīryaṃ śobhanavīryaṃ dhanam |
taddeturbhavasi |
grāvaḥ yāḥ stutivācaḥ santi tāḥ tava svabhūtāḥ |
he mitramahaḥ hitakāritajaḥ sajātyam asmākaṃ bāndhavaṃ bhajasa ityarthaḥ |
he agne āśuhemā āśu prerayitā tvaṃ svaśvyaṃ śobhanāśrvasamūhopetaṃ phalaṃ rariṣe dadāsi |
he agne puruvasuḥ prabhūtadhanaḥ tvaṃ nṛṇāṃ narāṇāṃ śardhaḥ balasthānīyaḥ asi |
7 |
tvama̭gne॒ rudrḙ a॒suro॒ maho̭ di॒vastvaṃ śardho̭ mā॒ruta॒ṃ pṛkṣa̭ ī॒śiṣḙ |
tvaṃ vā॒taira̭ru॒ṇairyā̭si॒ śaṃga̭yastvaṃ pū॒ṣā vi̭dha॒taḥ pā̭si॒ nu tmanā̭ ||
6 ||
tvam | agne | rudraḥ | asuraḥ | mahaḥ | divaḥ | tvam | śardhaḥ | mārutam | pṛkṣaḥ | īśiṣe | tvam | vātaiḥ | aruṇaiḥ | yāsi | śaṃgayaḥ | tvam | pūṣā | vidhataḥ | pāsi | nu | tmanā ||6||
1. va-bahuprajākārayā |
2. tha-saṃbhujavyāpakam; ka-ma-va-satatabhujavyāpakam; ṇa-na-satataṃ bhujavyāpakam |
he ^agne ^tvaṃ ^maho ^divaḥ mahato dyulokāt |
upalakṣaṇametat |
lokatrayāt |
^asuraḥ śatrūṇāṃ nirasitā ^rudraḥ asi |
ruhaḥ khaṃ duḥkheturvā pāpādiḥ |
tasya drāvayitā etannāmako devo'si |
‘rudro vā eṣa yadagniḥ’ (tai. saṃ. 5. 4. 3.1) ityādiṣvagneḥ rudraśabdena vyavahārāt |
yadvā |
tvaṃ rudraḥ |
rauti māmaniṣṭvā narā duḥkhai patiṣyantīti rudraḥ |
tādṛśo'si |
‘rudro rautīti sataḥ’ (niru. 10. 5) iti hi niruktam |
tathā maho mahato dyulokasya sambandhī asuro'si |
asurabalam |
tasya dātā ādityarūpaśca tvamasi |
^agnerdyulokasambandhyasuratvam ‘uta vā divo asurasya manma’ (ṛ. saṃ. 5.41.3) ityādiṣu |
tathā ^tvaṃ ^māruta ^śardhaḥ marutsamūha rūpaṃ balamasi |
vāyurūpaśca tvamasītyarthaḥ |
evam agnyādityavāyurūpastvam |
^vṛkṣaḥ agnasya havilakṣaṇasya tvam ^īśiṣe īśvaro bhavasi |
yasmādevaṃ ^tatvaṃ ^vātaiḥ vāyusadṛśaiḥ ^aruṇaiḥ aruṇavarṇairaśvaiḥ ^aṃgayaḥ sukhasya gṛharūpa āvāsabhūtaḥ san ^yāsi prāpnopi yāgagṛham |
yadvā |
aruṇairgamanaśīlairvātairvāyubhiḥ nunnaḥ ^manu yāsi vane |
tathā ^tvaṃ ^pūpā sarvasya poṣakaḥ ^san ^nu kṣipraṃ ^dhamanā ātmanā anugraharūpayā svabuddhayaiva ^vidhataḥ paricarato yajamānān ^pāsi ^||

7 ||
tvam | agne | draviṇaḥ'dāḥ | aram'kṛte | tvam | devaḥ | savitā | ratna'dhāḥ | asi | tvam | bhargaḥ | nṛ'pate | vasvaḥ | īśiṣe | tvam | pāyuḥ | dame | yaḥ | te | avidhat || 7 ||
he ^agne ^tvam ^araṃkṛte tvāmalaṃkurvate yajamānāya ^draviṇodāḥ hiraṇyasya ^dātā ^asi |
^devaḥ dyotamānaḥ ^savitā ^sarvasya satkarmasu prerakaḥ ^tvaṃ ^ratnadhāḥ ramaṇīyānāṃ dhanānāṃ maṇivuktādīnāṃ dhārayitā ^asi |
tathā he ^nṛpate nṛṇāṃ pālakāgne ^tvaṃ ^bhagaḥ bhajanīyaḥ san ^vasvaḥ vasunaḥ dhanasya ^īśiṣe samartho bhavasi dātum |
^yaḥ yajamānaḥ ^dame yajñagṛhe ^te tvām ^avidhat paricarati tasya ^pāyuḥ pālakaḥ ^tvam asi ||
tvāma̭gne da॒ma ā vi॒śapa̭tiṃ vi॒śastvā̭ rā॒jāna̭ṃ suvi॒draṃ ṛ̭ñjate |
tvaṃ viśvā̭ni sva॒nīka॒ patya̭se tvaṃ sa॒hasrā̭ṇi śa॒tā da॒śa pra॒ti ||
8 ||
tvām | agne | dame | ā | viśa'patim | viśaḥ | tvām | rājānam | su'vidram | ṛñjate | tvam | viśvāni | su'anīka | patyase | tvam | sahasrāṇi | śatā | daśa | prati || 8 ||
he ^agne ^viśapati yajamānānāṃ pālakaṃ ^tvāṃ ^viśaḥ yajamānāḥ ^dame svakīye yajñagṛhe ^ā āsādayanti ||
upasargavaśādyogakriyādhyāhāraḥ ||
tathā kṛtvā ^rājānaṃ dīpyamānaṃ ^suvidraṃ śobhanajñānam asmadanukūlacetaskaṃ sudhanaṃ vā ^tvām ^ṛñjate prasādhayanti ||
‘ṛñjatiḥ prasādhanakarmā’ iti
1. ca-ṇa1-va-utpannaḥ | 2. ṇa-ta-na-ma-tra1-poṣaṇaḥ | 3. ka-ma-subuddhayaiva | 4. ka-jha1-pāsa rakṣasi | 5. tha-ba-hiraṇyadātā | 6. ma-savitā sarvasya prasavitā |
yāskaḥ ||
yata evaṃ tato he ᵥsvanika śobhanajvālārūpasena ᵥtvaṃ ᵥviśvāni sarvāṇi |
havīṃpīti śeṣaḥ ||
ṣaṣṭhyarthe dvitīyā ||
viśveṣāṃ havipām ājyasomapuroḍāśādīnāṃ ᵥpadyase īśvaro bhavasi ||
īśvaratāmaitat |
‘irajyati padyate’ ( ni. 2. 21. 2. ) iti tannāmasu pāṭhāt ||
yadvā patibhāvamācarasi |
ᵥtvaṃ tu ᵥsahasrāṇi ᵥśatā śatāni ᵥdaśa etatsa~khyākāni ᵥprati |
etatsa~khyākānāmimatasādhanānāṃ prati- nidhirityarthaḥ ||
‘pratiḥ pratinidhipratidānayoḥ’ ( pā. sū. 1. 4. 92 ) iti karmapravacanīyatvam |
tairyatphalaṃ bhavati tattvayā labhyata ityarthaḥ ||
tvāma̭gne॒ pita̭ra॒ṃmiṣṭi̭bhi॒rnastva̭ṃ ā॒trāya̭ śa॒myā ta॒nurṷcam |
tvaṃ pu॒tro bha̭vasi॒ yastḙ'vi॒dhattvaṃ sa॒khā su॒śeva̭: pā॒syādhṛ̭ṣaḥ ||
9 ||
tvām | a॒gne | pi॒ta॒ram | i॒ṣṭi॒'bhi̭ḥ | na॒ḥ | tvām | ā॒trāya̭ | śa॒myā | ta॒nuḥ'rṷcam || tvam | pu॒traḥ | bha॒va॒si॒ | yaḥ | te॒ | a॒vi॒dhat | tvam | sa॒khā | su॒'śeva̭ḥ | pā॒si | ā॒'dhṛṣa̭ḥ || 9 ||
he ᵥagne ᵥpitaraṃ pālakaṃ ᵥvāṃ ᵥnaraḥ netāro yajamānāḥ ᵥiṣṭibhiḥ : eṣaṇasādhanairyajñairyajanta iti śeṣaḥ |
pitṛtpālanāya yajanta ityarthaḥ |
tathā ᵥtanūrucaṃ śarīrāṇāṃ dīpakaṃ śarīreṣu dīpyamānaṃ vaiśvā- naradhmanā ᵥtvāṃ ᵥātrāya ātratvāya saubhrātrāya ᵥśamyā ||
karmanāmaiṭat ||
karmaṇā yajanta iti śeṣaḥ |
yadvā |
ubhayatra vakṣyamāṇena ‘avidhat’ ityanena saṃbandhaḥ |
he agne ᵥyaḥ naraḥ ᵥte tvām ᵥavidhat paricarati tasya ᵥtvaṃ ᵥputro ᵥbhavasi putravatpālayitā bhavasi |
tathā ᵥtvaṃ ᵥsakhā samānasyānaḥ sakhivaddhitakārī ᵥsuśevaḥ ||
śevamiti’ sukhanāma ||
suṣṭhu sukhakārī san ᵥādhṛṣaḥ : ādharṣakāmā ᵥpāsirakṣasi |
pitṛtkāmāya pitṛtvena ātratkāmāya ātratvena putravakāmāya’ putrasthānīyo bhavasi |
triprakāreṇa rakṣasītyarthaḥ ||
tvama̭gne॒ ṛbhṷrā॒ke na̭ma॒syastvaṃ vā̭jasya॒ kṣuma̭tau॒ rāya̭ ī̭śiṣe |
tvaṃ vi bhā̭sya॒nu da̭kṣi॒ dāva̭ne॒ tvaṃ vi̭śi॒śura̭si॒ yajña̭māta॒niḥ ||
10 ||
tvam | a॒gne | ṛ॒bhuḥ | ā॒ke | na॒ma॒syaḥ | tvam | vā॒jasya̭ | kṣu॒'ma̭taḥ | rā॒yaḥ | ī॒śi॒ṣe | tvam | vi | bhā॒si | a॒nu | dha॒kṣi | dā॒va॒ne | tvam | vi॒'śi॒śaḥ | a॒si | yajña̭m | ā॒ta॒niḥ || 10 ||
he ᵥagne ᵥtvam ᵥṛbhuḥ |
urubhāsamāno medhāvī vā |
ᵥtvam ᵥāke antike saṃnidhāveva ᵥnamasyaḥ : namaskāryaḥ |
stutya ityarthaḥ |
itaradevatāvata mantrapratipādyākāreṇa’ stutyo na bhavasi kiṃtu pratyakṣa ityarthaḥ |
tathā ᵥtvaṃ ᵥkṣumataḥ śabdavataḥ sarvatra śrūyamāṇasya ᵥvājasya annasya ᵥrāyaḥ : dhanasya ca ᵥīśiṣe svāmī bhavasi |
atastadehītyarthaḥ |
he agne ᵥtvaṃ ᵥvi bhāsi viśeṣeṇa dīpyase |
tadartham
1. ta-vā-śeva iti |
2. ṇa-ta-na-putrakāmāya |
3. ka-la-ca-kṣa-ṇa-mantrapratipādyakāreṇa |

‘nūṃnaṃ mā te’ ityeṣā tasyānte śaṃsanīyā |

21 ||
nūṃnam | sā | te | prati | varam | jaritre | duhīyat | indra | dakṣiṇā | maghonī | śikṣā | stotṛbhyaḥ | mā | ati | dhṛk | bhagaḥ | naḥ | bṛhat | vadema | vidathai | suvīrāḥ ||21||
he indra yā dakṣiṇā |

tādṛśīṃ dakṣiṇāṃ stotṛbhyaḥ asmabhyaṃ śikṣā prayaccha |

1 ||

5. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ṇa 1-tatha-na-va-jāta |
yaḥ pṛthivīṃ vyathamānāmahaṃhat parvatānprakupitāṃ aramṇāt | yo antarikṣaṃ vimame varīyo yo dyā mastambhātsa janāsa indraḥ || 2 || yaḥ | pṛthivīm | vyathamānām | ahaṃhat | yaḥ | parvatān | prakupitān | aramṇāt | yaḥ | antarikṣam | vimame | varīyaḥ | yaḥ | dyām | astambhāt | saḥ | janāsaḥ | indraḥ ||2||
he janāḥ yaḥ indraḥ vyathamānāṃ calantīṃ pṛthivīm ahaṃhat śarkarādibhirdṛḍhāmakarot ||
‘dṛ hadi dṛddhau’ ||

yo hatvāhimariṇātsapta sindhūnyo gā udājadapadhā valasya | yo aśmanorantaragniṃ jajāna saṃvṛksamatsu sa janāsa indraḥ || 3 || yaḥ | hatvā | ahim | ariṇāt | sapta | sindhūn | yaḥ | gāḥ | ut | ājat | apadhā | valasya | yaḥ | aśmanoḥ | antaḥ | agnim | jajāna | sam | vṛk | samatsu | saḥ | janāsaḥ | indraḥ ||3||

yaṃ kranda̭sī॒ saṃya̭tī॒ vihva̭yete॒ parḙ'va॒ra u॒bhayā̭ a॒mitrā̭: |

8 |
yam | krandasī | iti | saṃyatī iti sam'yatī | vihvayete iti vi'hvayete | pare | avare | ubhayā: | amitrā: | samānam | cit | ratham | ātasthivāṃsā | nānā | havete iti | sa: | janāsa: | indra: || 8 ||
yaṃ krandasī rodasī śabdaṃ kurvāṇe mānuṣī daive ca dve sene vā saṃyatī parasparaṃ saṃgacchantyau yamindraṃ vihvayete svarakṣārthaṃ vividhamāhvayata: |
pare utkṛṣṭā: avare adhamāśca ubhayā: ubhayavidhā: amitrā: śatrava: yamāhvayanti |
samānam indrarathasadṛśaṃ ratham ātasthivāṃsā āsthitau dvau rathinau tamevedaṃ nānā pṛthak pṛthak havete āhvayete |

1. ma-ya: kṛśasya |
2. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ṇa-1-tha-na-ubhayamāhvayanti ; jha-1-ubhayavidhamāhvayanti |
yasmānna ṛte vijayante janāso yaṃ yudhyamānā avase havante | yo viśvasya pratimānaṃ babhūva yo acyutacyutsa janāsa indraḥ || 9 || yasmāt | na | ṛte | vi'jayante | janāsaḥ | yam | yudhyamānāḥ | avase | havante | yaḥ | viśvasya | prati'mānam | babhūva | yaḥ | acyut'cyut | saḥ | janāsaḥ | indraḥ || 9 ||
ᵛyasmāt ᵛṛte ᵛjanāsaḥ janāḥ ᵛna ᵛvijayante vijayaṃ na prāpnuvanti |
ataḥ ᵛyudhyamānāḥ yuddhaṃ kurvoṇā janāḥ ᵛavase svarakṣaṇāya ᵛyam indraṃ ᵛhavante āhvayanti |
ᵛyaḥ ca ᵛviśvasya sarvasya jagataḥ ᵛpratimānaṃ pratinidhiḥ ᵛbabhūva |
ᵛyaḥ ca ᵛacyutacyut acyutānāṃ kṣayarahitānāṃ parvatādīnāṃ cyāvayitā ᵛsaḥ ᵛindraḥ ityādi prasiddham ||
yaḥ śaśvato maheno dadhānānmanyamānāñcharvī jaghāna | yaḥ śardhate nānudadāti śrudhyāṃ yo dasyurhantā sa janāsa indraḥ || 10 || yaḥ | śaśvataḥ | māha | enaḥ | dadhānān | amaṃyamānān | śarvīm | jaghāna | yaḥ | śardhate | na | anudadāti | śrudhyām | yaḥ | dasyoḥ | hantā | saḥ | janāsaḥ | indraḥ || 10||
ᵛyaḥ ᵛmahi mahat ᵛenaḥ pāpaṃ ᵛdadhānān ᵛśaśvataḥ bahūn ᵛamanyamānān ātmānamajānata indramapūjayato vā janān ᵛśarvīm |
śraṇāti śatrūnaneti śarvañjaḥ |
tenāyudhena ᵛjaghāna ||
hanterliṭi rūpam ||
ᵛyaḥ ca ᵛśardhate utsāhaṃ kurvate anātmajñāya janāya ᵛśrudhyām utsāhanīyaṃ karma ᵛnānudadāti na prayacchati ||
anupūrvān 'hudāñ dāne' jauhotyādikaḥ |
'abhyastānāmādiḥ' iti 'tiṅi codāttavati' iti gaternighātaḥ |

8 ||
yaḥ śambaraṃ parvateṣu kṣiyantaṃ catvāriṃśyāṃ śaradyanvavindat | ojāyamānaṃ yo ahiṃ jaghāna dānuṃ śayānaṃ sa janāsa indraḥ || 11 || yaḥ | śambaram | parvateṣu | kṣiyantam | catvāriṃśyām | śaradi | anu'avindat | ojāyamānam | yaḥ | ahim | jaghāna | dānum | śayānam | saḥ | janāsaḥ | indraḥ || 11 ||
ᵛyaḥ ᵛparvateṣu ᵛkṣiyantam indrabhiyā bahūn saṃvatsarān pracchanno bhūtvā parvataguhāsu nivasantaṃ ᵛśambaram etannāmakaṃ māyāvinamasuraṃ ᵛcatvāriṃśyāṃ ᵛśaradi catvāriṃśe saṃvatsare ᵛanvavindat anvidhyālabhata |
labdhvā ca ᵛyaḥ ᵛojāyamānam ||

balamācarantam ᵛahim ahantāraṃ ᵛdānuṃ dānavaṃ ᵛśayānaṃ śambaramuraṃ ᵛjaghāna hatavān ᵛsaḥ ᵛindraḥ nāhamiti ||
ṛ. 2-7
yaḥ sapta̭raśmi॒rvṛṣa̭bhastu॒viṣma̭nā॒vāsa̭sṛ॒tave॒ sapta̭ sindhūn | yo rauhi̭ṇamasphura॒dvajra̭bāha॒ryāro̭ha॒ntaṃ sa ja̭nāsa॒ indra̭: || 12 || yaḥ | sapta̭-raśmiḥ | vṛṣa̭bhaḥ | tu॒viṣma̭ān | ava̭-sṛjat | sa॒pta'i̭ve | sapta̭ | sindhūn | yaḥ | rauhi̭ṇam | asphurat | vajra̭-bāhaḥ | dyām | ā'ro̭hantam | saḥ | ja̭nāsaḥ | indra̭: || 12 ||
yaḥ 'saptaraśmiḥ saptasaṃkhyākāḥ parjanyā raśmayo yasya |

'yaḥ 'somapāḥ somasya pātā 'nicitaḥ sarvaiḥ |

'vajrabāhuḥ vajrasadabāhuḥ |
'yaḥ ca 'vajrahastaḥ vajrayuktaḥ 'saḥ 'indraḥ ityādi prasiddham ||
yaḥ su॒nvanta̭mavati॒ yaḥ pa̭ca॒ntaṃ yaḥ śaṃsa̭nta॒ṃ yaḥ śaśa̭mānamūtau | yasya॒ brahma॒ vardha̭na॒ṃ yasya॒ somo॒ yasye॒daṃ rādha॒ḥ sa ja̭nāsa॒ indra̭: || 14 || yaḥ | su॒nvanta̭m | a॒vati | yaḥ | pa॒ca॒ntam | yaḥ | śaṃsa̭ntam | yaḥ | śaśa̭mānam | ū॒tau | yasya̭ | brahma̭ | vardha̭nam | yasya̭ | soma̭: | yasya̭ | i॒dam | rādha̭ḥ | saḥ | ja̭nāsaḥ | indra̭: || 14 ||
'yaḥ 'sunvantaṃ somābhiṣavaṃ kurvantaṃ yajamānam 'avati rakṣati |
'yaḥ ca puroḍāśādīni havīṃṣi 'pacantaṃ 'yaḥ ca 'ūtī ūtaye ||
'supāṃ suluk' iti caturthyāḥ pūrvasavarṇadīrghaḥ |

1. jha-ma-samaraśmayaḥ |
2. tha-yaḥ |
śaṃsantaṃ yaḥ ca śaśamānam avati stotraṃ kurvāṇaṃ rakṣati |
brahma parivṛḍhaṃ stotraṃ yasya vardhanaṃ vṛddhikaraṃ bhavati |
tathā yasya somaḥ vṛddherheturbhavati |
yasya ca idam asmadīyaṃ rādhaḥ puroḍāśādilakṣaṇamatra vṛddhikaraṃ bhavati |
saḥ indraḥ ityādi pramiddham ||
yaḥ sunvate pacate dudha ā cidvāaṃ dardarṣi sa kilāsi mṛtyaḥ |
vayaṃ te indra viśvaha priyāsaḥ suvīrāso vidathamā vadema ||
15 ||
yaḥ | sunvate | pacate | dudhaḥ | ā | cit | vājam | dardarṣi | saḥ | kila | asi | satyaḥ | vayam | te | indra | viśvaha | priyāsaḥ | suvīrāsaḥ | vidatham | ā | vadema || 15 ||
idānīmṛṣiḥ sākṣātkṛtamindraṃ prati brūte |
he indra yaḥ dudhaḥ durdharaḥ san sunvate somābhipavaṃ kurvate puroḍāśādihavīṃṣi pacate yajamānāya vājam annaṃ balaṃ vā ā dardarṣi bhṛśaṃ prāpayasi saḥ
sa॒dhī॒mā ya̭nti॒ pari̭ vi॒bhra̭ntī॒ḥ payo̭ vi॒śva॒psnyā̭ya॒ pra bhara̭nta॒ bhojana̭m | sa॒mā॒no a॒dhvā pra॒vatā̭nu॒ṣyade॒ yastā̭kṛ॒ṇoḥ pra॒thama̭ṃ sā॒syukthya̭ḥ || 2 || sa॒dhī | ī̭m | ā | ya॒nti॒ | pari̭ | vi॒bhra̭ntīḥ | pa॒rya̭ | vi॒śva॒psnyā̭ya | pra | bha॒ra॒nta | bhojana̭m | sa॒māna̭ḥ | a॒dhvā | pra॒vatā̭m | a॒nu॒'syadḙ | yaḥ | tā | a॒kṛ॒ṇoḥ | pra॒thama̭m | saḥ | a॒si | u॒kthya̭ḥ ||2||
^sadhī |
atrateluk chāndasaḥ |
‘ sahasya sadhiḥ ’ iti sadhyādeśo'ntodāttatvena nipātitaḥ |
sadhīcīnāḥ ^payaḥ udakaṃ ^pari paritaḥ ^vibhrantīḥ bibhrāṇāḥ ^īm enā nadyaḥ ^ā ^yanti sarvato gacchanti |

^pra ^bharanta prakarṣeṇa saṃpādayanti |
kuta etat tadāha |
^pravatāṃ pravaṇavatāṃ nimnagānāṃ payasām ^anuṣyade |
‘ kṛmyarthe tavaikentyantvanaḥ ’ iti kenpratyayaḥ |
^anuṣyandanīyaḥ |
^adhvā mārgaḥ ^samānaḥ eko hi |
^yastā tāni somotpādananadīpravartanādīni karmāṇi ^prathamaṃ pūrvam ^akṛṇoḥ akaroḥ |
^saḥ tādṛśamahimopetastvam ^ukthyaḥ ^asi sarvaiḥ praśasyo bhavasi ||
a॒nve॒ko va̭dati॒ yahdā̭ti॒ tadrṷ̄pā॒ mina̭nta॒darpā॒ eka̭ ī̭yate | vi॒śvā eka̭sya॒ vinṷda॒stiti̭kṣate॒ yastā̭kṛ॒ṇoḥ pra॒thama̭ṃ sā॒syukthya̭ḥ || 3 || anṷ | eka̭ḥ | va॒dati̭ | yat | da॒dāti̭ | tat | rū॒pā | mi॒nan | tat'a̭pāḥ | eka̭ḥ | ī̭yate | vi॒śvā | eka̭sya | vi'nṷdaḥ | ti॒ti̭kṣate | yaḥ | tā | a॒kṛ॒ṇoḥ | pra॒thama̭m | saḥ | a॒si | u॒kthya̭ḥ ||3||
yajamānaḥ ^yat haviḥ devebhyaḥ ^dadāti ^tat ^ekaḥ hotā ^anu ^vadati |
yājyāpuronuvākyābhyāṃ devatāḥ smārayati |
^ekaḥ adhvaryuḥ ^rūpā paścādīnāṃ rūpāṇi ^minan hiṃsan ^tadapāḥ tat viśasanā- hyam apaḥ karma yasya tādṛśaḥ san ^īyate devayajane sarvatra gacchati |
^ekasya adhvaryoḥ ^viśvāḥ ^vinudaḥ sarvāṇi tatkartṛkāṇi vikṣepaṇarūpāṇi karmavaiguṇyāni ^titikṣate |
tayoyaprāyaścittakaraṇena brahmā sahate ||
gusiñjikahyaḥ bhan kṣamāyāmiyyate ||
tāni haviṣpradānādīni karmāṇi ^yaḥ tvam ^akṛṇoḥ akārayaḥ ^saḥ ^tvam ^ukthyaḥ ^asi ||
pra॒jābhya̭ḥ pu॒ṣṭiṃ vi॒bharja̭nta ā॒sate॒ rayi̭miva pṛ॒ṣṭhaṃ a॒bhava̭ntamā॒yatḙ | a॒si॒nva॒nda॒staiḥ pi॒tu॒ratti̭ bhojana॒ṃ yastā̭kṛ॒ṇoḥ pra॒thama̭ṃ sā॒syukthya̭ḥ || 4 || pra॒jābhya̭ḥ | pu॒ṣṭim | vi॒'bharja̭ntaḥ | ā॒satḙ | rayi̭m'iva | pṛ॒ṣṭham | pra॒'bhava̭ntam | ā॒yatḙ | a॒si॒nvan | da॒staiḥ | pi॒tuḥ | atti̭ | bhojana̭m | yaḥ | tā | a॒kṛ॒ṇoḥ | pra॒thama̭m | saḥ | a॒si | u॒kthya̭ḥ || 4 ||
1. ma-ālayabhūtāya |
he indra ᵥpuṣṭiṃ tvayā dattaṃ poṣakaṃ dhanaṃ svakīyābhyaḥ ᵥprajābhyaḥ ᵥvibhajanta: asyaitāvadasyai- tāvaditi ᵥvibhāgaṃ kurvanto gṛhamedhina: ᵥāsate svasvagṛheṣu nivasanti |
tatra dṛṣṭānta: |
ᵥāyate gṛhaṃ pratyāgacchate'tithaye ᵥpṛṣṭhaṃ dhārakaṃ ᵥprabhavantaṃ bahubharaṇasamarthaṃ ᵥrayiṃ dhanaṃ yathā vibhajya prayacchanti tadvat |
ᵥasinvan setubandhādikaṃ karma kurvan loka: ᵥpitu: pālayityā diva: sakāśādāgataṃ ᵥbhojanam udakaṃ tatkāryā oṣadhī: ᵥdaṃṣṭrai: dantai: ᵥatti bhakṣayati |
yadvā asinvana anyāpriyamāṇo'bhi: pitu: pālakasya yajamānasya saṃbandhi bhojanaṃ havirlakṣaṇamannaṃ daṃṣṭraidantarūpairjvālairatti |
ᵥyastākṛṇo: ityādi siddham ||
adhākṛṇoḥ pṛthivīṃ saṃdaśe dive yo dhautīnāmahihanāriṇakpathaḥ |
taṃ tvā stomaibhirudabhirna vājinaṃ devaṃ devā ajanantasāsyukthyaḥ ||
5 ||
adha | akṛṇoḥ | pṛthivīm | sam'daśe | dive | yaḥ | dhautīnām | ahi'han | ariṇak | pathaḥ | tam | tvā | stomaibhiḥ | udabhiḥ | na | vājinam | devam | devāḥ | ajanan | saḥ | asi | ukthyaḥ || 5 ||
he indra ᵥadha api ca tvaṃ ᵥdive dyotanāya sūryāya ᵥpṛthivīṃ calitāṃ satīṃ dṛḍhīkṛtya ᵥsaṃdaśe saṃdarśanīyām ᵥakṛṇo: akaro: ||

ᵥsa: ityādi siddham ||
1. kha-ga-jha-1-ṇa-ta-na-va-asyaitāvaditi | 2. ka-jha-ma-pramidram | 3. kha-ga-ca-arecaya: | 4. kha-kāṣṭhāt | 5. ka-jha-ṇa-1-ma-madhurasopetam |
yaḥ pu॒ṣpiṇī̭śca॒ prasa̭vaśca॒ dharmā̭dhi dāne॒ vya̭va॒nīra̭dhārayaḥ | yaścā̭sa॒mā a̭rja॒no di॒dyuto̭ di॒va u॒rurvā̭ a॒bhita̭ḥ sā॒syukthya̭ḥ || 7 || yaḥ | pu॒ṣpiṇī̭ | ca | pra'sa̭vaḥ | ca | dharmā̭ | adhi̭ | dānḙ | vi | a॒va॒nīḥ | a॒dhā̭rayaḥ | yaḥ | ca॒ | a॒sa॒māḥ | a॒rja̭naḥ | di॒dyuta̭ḥ | di॒vaḥ | u॒ruḥ | ū॒rvān | a॒bhita̭ḥ | saḥ | a॒si | u॒kthya̭ḥ || 7 ||
he indra yaḥ tvaṃ puṣpiṇīḥ puṣpavatīḥ ca prasavāḥ prasūtāḥ prasūyamānā vā avanīḥ avitrī- ropadhīḥ dāne adhi ||
upalapyante masyāntyatreti dānaṃ kṣetram |
adhikaraṇe lyuṭ ||
tasmin kṣetre dharmā sakalalakṣaṇena karmaṇā ci adhārayaḥ nyadadhāḥ |
yaḥ ca tvaṃ divaḥ dyotamānasya sūryasya
he indra ᵛekasya ekākinaḥ śreṣṭhasya vā ᵛyasya te ᵛśrutau sukhanimitte tadarthaṃ ᵛjñātaṃ ᵛdaśa daśa śatāni harayo vāhanārthaṃ bhavanti |

te ca raśmayo varṣāsu ^utsam utsecanavantam ^udriṇam udakavantaṃ meghaṃ ^sākaṃ saha yugapadeva ^bahu ^sisicyuḥ |

sanā tā kā citrbhuvanā bhavītvā mādbhiḥ śaradbhirduro varanta vaḥ | aryatantā carato anyadanyadidyā cakāraṃ vayunā brahmaṇaspatiḥ || 5 || sanā | tā | kā | cit | bhuvanā | bhavītvā | mādbhiḥ | śaradbhiḥ | duraḥ | varanta | vaḥ | aryatantā | carataḥ | anyat'anyat | it | yā | cakāra | vayunā | brahmaṇaḥ | patiḥ || 5 ||
he ṛtvijyamānāḥ ^vaḥ yuṣmadarthaṃ ^sanā sanātanāni ^tā tāni ^kā ^cit kāni cit brahmaṇaspateḥ prajñānāni ^bhuvanā bhuvanānām |
‘supāṃ suluk’ iti ṣaṣṭhyā luk |
bhuvanāmudakānāṃ ^bhavītvā bhāvyānāṃ ^duraḥ dvārāṇi ^mādbhiḥ māsaiḥ |
‘pahna’ ityādinā māsaśabdasya māsa- bhāvaḥ |
‘svatavasmāsoṣāṃ ca ta iṣyate’ (pā. sū. 7. 4. 48. 2) iti tattvam ||
^śaradbhiḥ saṃvatsaraiśca ^varanta vivṛṇvanti |
tatkāle varṣaṇārthaṃ meghadvārāṇi udghāṭayantītyarthaḥ |
kāni punastāni |
^brahmaṇaspatiḥ ^yā yāni ^vayunā ||
jñānametat ||

tadanantaram ^ayatantā ayatamānau aprayatnau ubhau lokau cauśca pṛthivī ca ^anyadanyadit anyadanyadeva pṛthivī vṛṣṭijalaṃ divyamudakaṃ dyauḥ |
udakakāryaṃ havirlakṣaṇaṃ pārthivamajñam evaṃ parasparavyatihāreṇa ^carataḥ bhuñjate |
‘cara gatibhakṣaṇayoḥ’ ||
1 ||
abhinakṣanto abhi ye tamānuśunidhiṃ paṇīnāṃ paramaṃ guhā hitam | te vidvāṃsaḥ praticakṣyānṛtā punaryata u āyantadudīyurāviśam || 6 || abhinakṣantaḥ | abhi | ye | tam | ānuśuḥ | ni'dhim | paṇīnām | paramam | guhā | hitam | te | vidvāṃsaḥ | prati'cakṣya | anṛtā | punaḥ | yataḥ | ū~ iti | āyan | tat | ut | īyuḥ | ā'viśam || 6 ||
gosamūhe paṇibhirasurairapahṛte sati paṇīnāṃ sthānaṃ saramālyā devaśunyā jñātvā brahmaṇaspatinā sṛṣṭā aṅgirasastasthānaṃ prāpya gosamūhaṃ paṇibhinirmitā māyāśca dṛṣṭvā asuranivāsasthānaṃ svahastod- tenābhinā dagdhvā pratyāgacchan |
tadetat ṛcenocyate |
^abhinakṣantaḥ abhito gacchantaḥ ^ye aṅgirasaḥ
ᐡta ᐢnidhiṃ gosamūharūpaṃ nidhānaṃ dhanaṃ ᐣparamam utkṛṣṭaṃ ᐤpaṇīnām ||
tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī ||
paṇibhirguhāyāṃ nihitaṃ sthāpitam ᐥabhi ᐦānaśruḥ abhilakṣya ānaśire prāptavantaḥ |
ᐧvidvāṃsaḥ abhijñāḥ ᐨte aṅgirasaḥ ᐩanṛtā anṛtāni asuraiḥ kṛtā māyāḥ ᐪpraticakṣya dṛṣṭvā ᐫāviśam āveṣṭuṃ praveṣṭuṃ ᐬyata ᐭu yata eva sthānāt ᐮāyanū agacchan ᐯtat sthānaṃ ᐰpunaḥ ᐱudīyaḥ udagacchan |
utkarṣeṇa prāptāḥ ||

7 ||
ṛta'vānaḥ | prati'cakṣya | anṛtā | punaḥ | ā | ataḥ | ā | tasthuḥ | kavayaḥ | mahaḥ | pathaḥ | te | bāhu'bhyām | dhamitam | agnim | aśmani | nakiḥ | saḥ | asti | araṇaḥ | jahuḥ | hi | tam || 7 ||
ᐡṛtāvānaḥ |
ṛtamiti satyanāmadheyam |
tadvantaḥ ᐢkavayaḥ krāntadarśinaḥ sarvajñā aṅgirasaḥ ᐣanṛtā anṛtāni ᐤpraticakṣya pūrvoktaprakāreṇa dṛṣṭvā ᐥataḥ asmāt sthānāt ᐦpunaḥ ājigamivaḥ ᐧmahaspathaḥ mahato mārgān prati ᐨā ᐩtasthuḥ avatasthire |
avatiṣṭhamānāśca ᐪte ᐫbāhubhyāṃ hastābhyāṃ ᐬdhamitaṃ mathanotpāditaṃ prajvalitam ᐭagnim ᐮaśmani paṇīnāṃ nivāsabhūte śilocaye ᐯjahurhi dagdhuṃ tyaktavantaḥ khalu |
ᐰaraṇaḥ aramayitā dāhena duḥkhakārī ᐱsaḥ agniḥ pūrvaṃ tatra ᐲnakiḥ ᐳasti na vidyate |
avidyamānamagniṃ svasāmarthyāt utpādya tatra pravikṣapurityarthaḥ ||
ṛtajyena kṣipreṇa brahmaṇaspatyatra vaṣṭi pra tadaśnoti dhanvanā |
tasya sādhvīriṣavo yābhirasyati nṛcakṣaso dṛśye karṇayonayaḥ ||
8 ||
ṛta'jyena | kṣipreṇa | brahmaṇaḥ | patiḥ | yatra | vāṣṭi | pra | tat | aśnoti | dhanvanā | tasya | sādhvīḥ | iṣavaḥ | yābhiḥ | asyati | nṛ'cakṣasaḥ | dṛśye | karṇa'yonayaḥ || 8 ||
ṛtaṃ satyameva jyā yasya tena ᐡkṣipreṇa iṣūṇāṃ kṣepakeṇa ᐢdhanvanā dharaṇabhūtena dhanuṣā ᐣbrahmaṇa- spatiḥ ᐤbrahmaṇo mantrasya pālayitā devaḥ ᐥyatra yasmin vipaye ᐦvaṣṭi kāmayate ᐧtat ᐨpra ᐩaśnoti prakarṣeṇa vyāpnoti ||
aśnotyarthayena parasmaipadam ||
ᐪtasya dhanuṣaḥ ᐫsādhvīḥ sādhikāstāḥ ᐬiṣavaḥ bhavanti ᐭyābhiḥ iṣubhiḥ brahmaṇaspatiḥ ᐮasyati rākṣasādīn kṣipati |
kīdṛśyastāḥ |

upalakṣaṇametat |
sarvajñasya brahmaṇaspateḥ ᐰdṛśye darśanāya jñānāya jātāḥ ᐱkarṇayonayaḥ śrotrendriyeṇa grāhyā mantrabhūtāḥ ākarṇakṛtā vā bāṇāḥ |

1. ka-jha-ña-ṇa-ta-dha-ma-va-he brahmaṇaspate tvayā sṛṣṭāḥ brahmaṇaspatiḥ; kha-ga-pha-yaḥ brahmaṇaspatiḥ; na-he brahmaṇaspate tvayā sṛṣṭāḥ |
sa sa̭nyaḥ sa vi̭nyaḥ purohi̭taḥ sa suṣṭṷtaḥ sa yu॒dhi brahmaṇa॒spatiḥ |

9 ||
saḥ | sa॒m'nyaḥ | saḥ | vi॒'nyaḥ | pu॒raḥ'hi̭taḥ | saḥ | su॒'stṷtaḥ | saḥ | yu॒dhi | brahmaṇa̭ḥ | pa॒tiḥ | cākṣ‌maḥ | yat | vā॒jam | bhara̭te | ma॒tī | dhanā̭ | āt | it | sū॒ryaḥ | ta॒pa॒ti | ta॒pyatṷḥ | vṛ॒thā || 9 ||
˅purohitaḥ devaiḥ purato'tra sthāpitaḥ ˅saḥ ˅brahmaṇaspatiḥ ˅sanyaḥ viśliṣṭānāṃ mantraiḥ saṃgamayitā bhavati |
˅vinayaḥ saṃgatānāṃ vividhaṃ netā pṛthakkartāpi ˅saḥ eva |
tathā ˅sa ˅suṣṭutaḥ śobhanaṃ stotṛbhiḥ stutaśca bhavati |
tathā ˅yudhi saṃgrāme ˅saḥ stotṛṇāmanugrahāya prādurbhavatīti śeṣaḥ |
˅yat yadā ˅cākṣ‌maḥ ||
caṣṭeḥ kṣamatervā ||
sarvasya draṣṭā sarvasaho vā brahmaṇaspatiḥ ˅vājam annaṃ ˅matī mananīyaṃ praśastaṃ ˅dhanā dhanaṃ ca stotṛṇāmarthāya ˅bharate bibharti vṛddhyārā poṣayati |
˅ādit anantarameva ˅tapyatuḥ tāpakaḥ ˅sūryaḥ ˅vṛthā |
anāyāsavacanametat |
anāyāsena ˅tapati dīpyate |
brahmaṇaspatinā dattairdhaneśca yajñasya siddhatvāt svayaṃ kevalaṃ havīṃṣi bhuñjāna eva vartate ityarthaḥ ||
vibhu prabhu prathamaṃ medhanāva̭to bṛhaspateḥ suvi॒datrā̭ṇi rā॒dhyā |

10 ||
vi॒bhu | pra॒bhu | pra॒tha॒mam | me॒dha॒nā'va̭taḥ | bṛhaspatḙḥ | su॒vi॒datrā̭ṇi | rā॒dhyā | i॒mā | sātā̭ni | ve॒nyasya̭ | vā॒jina̭ḥ | yena̭ | janā̭: | u॒bhayḙ | bhu॒ñjatḙ | viśa̭ḥ || 10 ||
˅vibhu vibhūti vyāptāni ˅prabhu prabhūni prauḍhāni ˅prathamaṃ prathamāni mukhyāni evaṃbhūtāni ˅medhanāvataḥ varṇavato vṛṣṭipradasya ˅bṛhaspateḥ bṛhato mantrasya pālayituḥ saṃbandhini ˅suvidatrāṇi ||
‘vidu lābhe’ |

suṣṭu labdhavyāni |

tṛtīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī ||
brahmaṇaspatinā dattāni |
˅yena anena ˅ubhaye ubhayavidhāḥ ˅janāḥ stotāro yajamānāśca devā manuṣyāśca vā ˅viśaḥ niviṣṭāḥ santaḥ ˅bhuñjate bhogān labhante |
tatsarvaṃ brahmaṇampatinā datta- mityarthaḥ ||
2 ||
yo'va̭re vṛ॒janḙ vi॒śvathā̭ vi॒bhurmahā̭mu॒ raṇa̭ḥ śa॒rdhamā̭ va॒vakṣi̭tha |

yaḥ | a॒va॒re | vṛ॒janḙ | vi॒śva'thā̭ | vi॒bhuḥ | ma॒hām | ū~ iti̭ | ra॒ṇaḥ | śa॒rdhamā̭ | va॒vakṣi̭tha | saḥ | de॒vaḥ | de॒vān | pra॒ti | pa॒pra॒the | pṛ॒thu | vi॒śvā | it | ū~ iti̭ | tā | pa॒ri'bhūḥ | brahmaṇa̭ḥ | pa॒tiḥ || 11 ||
vviśvathā sarvaprakāreṇa vvibhuḥ vyāsaḥ vraṇaḥ ramayitā stotavyo vā vyaḥ vbrahmaṇaspatiḥ vacare nikṛṣṭe vvrajane varjanahetubhūte bale vartamānam |

yaśasā vistīrṇo bhavatīti yāvat |

he vmaghavānā |

‘mīḍ hiṃsāyāṃ’ |
kaiyādikaḥ |

uta | āśiṣprāḥ | anu | śṛṇvanti | vahyaḥ | sabheyaḥ | vipraḥ | bharate | matī | dhanā | vīḻudveṣāḥ | anu | vaśam | ṛṇam | ā'dadiḥ | saḥ | ha | vājī | sam | it | the | brahmaṇaḥ | patiḥ || 13 ||
vuta api ca vāśiṣprāḥ āśutarāḥ śīghragāminaḥ | vvayaḥ | aśnāmaitat | voḍhāro brahmaṇasteraśvāḥ vanu vśṛṇvanti | asmābhiḥ kṛtaṃ stotramanukrameṇa śṛṇvanti | yadvā brahmaṇaspatinā
1. kha-ga-gha-1-rūpajālam |
2. kha-ga-jha-ṇa-ga-1-ta-tha-na-ma-va-ekapadyam |
kṛtamanuśāsanaṃ śṛṇvanti |
ataste tamasmadīyaṃ yajñaṃ prāpayantviti śeṣa: |
vsabheya: sabhāyāṃ sādhu: ||
‘cchandasi’ iti ṭha: ||
vvipra: medhāvī adhvaryurhotā vā vmatī mayā mananīyena stotreṇa ||
‘supāṃ suluka’ iti pūrvasavarṇadīrgha: ||
vdhanā havilakṣaṇāni dhanāni tasmai brahmaṇaspataye vbharate bibharti |
saṃpādayatīti yāvat |
yadvā stotreṇa dhanāni bharate bibharti poṣayati |
ataḥ vbīludveṣā: |
bīḻūn dṛḍhān rākṣasādīn dveṣṭīti tādṛśo brahmaṇaspati: vvaśā vaśāyā: ||
‘supāṃ suluka’ iti prakṛterluk |
‘ṛtyaka:’ iti prakṛtibhāva: ||
vṛṇam asmābhiryajñabhi: pradeyamavadānātmakam anukrameṇa vādadi: ādātā bhavatīti śeṣa: |
avadānasya ṛṇatvaṃ ca taittirīye ‘tribhirṛṇavā jāyate brahmacaryeṇa ṛṣibhya: ’ (tai. saṃ. 6. 3. 10. 5) ityādinā spaṣṭamājñātam |
yadvā anu: ānuguṇya |
vaśā vaśasya kāmasyābhilāpasya ānuguṇyamādātā bhavatīti yojyam |
haśabda: prasiddhau |
vsa: khalu vbrahmaṇaspati: vsamithe |
saṃyanti saṃgacchante'sminnāhutibhirdevā iti samitho yajña: |
tasmin vvājī annavān |
tasmādvipa ādātā bhavatītyartha: ||
brahmaṇaspatairabhavadyathāvaśaṃ satyo munyurmahi karmā kariṣyataḥ | yo gā udājatsa dive vi cābhajanmahiva rīti: śarvasāsatpṛthak || 14 || brahmaṇa: | pate: | abhavat | yathā'vaśam | satya: | manyu: | mahi | karma | kariṣyataḥ | ya: | gā: | ut'ājat | sa: | dive | vi | ca | abhajat | mahī'iva | rīti: | śarvasā | asat | pṛthak || 14 ||
vmahi mahat vkarma vkariṣyataḥ vbrahmaṇaspate: vmanyu: mananasādhano mantra: krodho vā vyathāvaśaṃ yathākāmaṃ vsatya: avitatha: vabhavat |
sa yathā kāmayate tathā karotītyartha: |
vya: brahmaṇaspati: paṇibhirapahatā: vgā: vudājat udagamayat |
guhāyā niragamayadityartha: |
vsa: brahmaṇaspati: vdive dyulokāya tatratyebhyo devebhya ityartha: |
vvi vabhajat ca tā gā vibhaktāścākarot |
sa ca gosamūha: vmahīva vrīti: |
mahatī stutiriva ’ ||
‘rīṅ sravaṇe’ ||
yathā gaṅgādīnāṃ sroto vibhaktaṃ sat nimnadeśā- bhimukhaṃ prasarati tathā vśavasā ātmīyena balena tattadevagṛhaṃ vpṛthak vibhibhram vasat agacchat ||
brahmaṇaspate suyamasya viśvahā rāya: syāma rathyo3 vayasvata: | vīreṣu vīrā~ upa pṛṇdhi nastvaṃ yadīśāno brahmaṇo verṣi me havam || 15 || brahmaṇa: | pate | su'yamasya | viśvahā | rāya: | syāma | rathya: | vayasvata: | vīreṣu | vīrān | upa | pṛṇdhi | na: | tvam | yat | īśāna: | brahmaṇa: | verṣi | me | havam || 15 ||
he vbrahmaṇaspate mantrādhipate vsuyamasya suṣṭhu niyantavyasya vvayasvata: annayuktasya vrāya: dhanasya vviśvahā viśveṣu sarveṣvaha:su vrathya: adhipataya: vsyāma bhūyāsma |
rathā eṣāṃ santīti rathya: ||
1. kha-ga-anu anukrameṇa; ṇa-anu anukrameṇa |
2. ka-ca-jha-ṇa-tha-ma-va-stutiriva |
3. ka-ca-jha- ta-tha-ma-va-śravaṇe |
‘ chandasīvanipau ’ iti matvarthīya īkāraḥ |
api ca ᵥnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵥvīreṣu vīryājāteṣvasmadīyeṣu putreṣu ᵥvīrān putrān ᵥtvam ᵥupa ᵥpṛṇdhi saṃpṛktān kuru utpādaya |
‘ pṛcī saṃparke ’ |
raudhādikaḥ |
vayaṃ pautrānapi labhemahīti yāvat |
ᵥīśānaḥ sarvasyeśvarastvaṃ ᵥbrahmaṇā |
annanāmaiṭan |
havirlakṣaṇe- nānnena saha ᵥme ᵥhavaṃ madīyaṃ stutilakṣaṇamāhvānaṃ ᵥvepi kāmayase |

he ᵥbrahmaṇaspate ᵥyantā sarvasya jagato niyantā ᵥtvamasya ᵥsūktasya idaṃ sūktam |
‘ kriyāgrahaṇaṃ kartavyam ’ iti karmaṇaḥ saṃpradānatvāt caturthyarthe ṣaṣṭhī |
ᵥbodhi budhasva |
buddhā ᵥtanayaṃ putraṃ ᵥca asmabhyaṃ ᵥjinva preraya |
jiviḥ prīṇanārtho'tra preraṇe vartate dhātūnāmekārthatvāt |
ᵥyat ᵥbhadraṃ kalyāṇaṃ ᵥdevāḥ sarve ᵥavanti rakṣanti ᵥviśvaṃ sarvaṃ ᵥtat bhadraṃ ca jinveti śeṣaḥ |
vayaṃ ca ᵥsuvīrāḥ śobhanairviraiḥ putrapautrādibhirupetaḥ santaḥ ᵥbṛhat prauḍhaṃ stotraṃ ᵥvidathe |
vidanti yajñavyatayā asmin devān jānantīti vidatho yajñaḥ |
tasmin ᵥvadema bravāma |
3 |
‘ indhānaḥ ’ iti pañcacaṃ tṛtīyaṃ sūktaṃ gāyatramadaṃ jāgataṃ brāhmaṇaspatyam |
tathā cānukrāntam— ‘ indhānaḥ pañca jāgataṃ tu ’ iti |
laiṅgiko viniyogaḥ |
indhāno agniṃ vanavadanuṣyataḥ kṛtabrahmā śṛṇuvadrātahavya ita |
jātena jñātamati sa pra sarsṛte yaṃ yujaṃ kṛṇute brahmaṇaspatiḥ |
1 |
indhānaḥ | agnim | vanavat | vanuṣyataḥ | kṛta'brahmā | śṛṇuvat | rāta'havyaḥ | ita | jātena | jñātam | ati | saḥ | pra | sarsṛte | yam | 'yama | yujam | kṛṇute | brahmaṇaḥ | patiḥ || 1 ||
ᵥagnim ᵥindhānaḥ brahmaṇaspateryāgārthamagniṃ prajvalayan ᵥyajamānaḥ ᵥvanuṣyataḥ hiṃsiyato hiṃsāṃ kurvato vā śatrūn ᵥvanavat vanuyāt hiṃsyāt |

ᵥit iti cārthe |
ᵥkṛtabrahmā |
brahma stotraṃ kṛtaṃ yena sa tathoktaḥ |

rātaṃ dattaṃ havyaṃ haviyena |
evaṃbhūtaḥ san ᵥśṛṇuvat vardhate |
‘ duośri gativṛddhayoḥ ’ |
asmāṇṇyantāt luṅi caḍi3 rūpam |

pautramapi paśyan cirakālaṃ jīvedityarthaḥ |
ᵥsaṃtairdhulgantāt vyatyayenātmanepadam |

tathā ᐞyajet brahmaṇaspatirhaviṣā iṣṭavāneva ᐞyajyayoḥ ayajvanaḥ ᐞbhojanaṃ bhogasādhanaṃ ᐞtri ᐞbhajāti viśeṣeṇa bhajeta' ityarthaḥ ||
yaja̭sva vīra॒ pra vi̭hi manāya॒to bha॒draṃ ma̭ne॒: kṛṇuṣva vṛtratūryḙ | ha॒viṣkṛ̭ṇuṣva subha॒go yathā̭sasi॒ brahma̭ṇa॒spate॒ra ā vṛ̭ṇīmahe || 2 || yaja̭sva | vīra̭ | pra | vi̭hi | ma॒nāya॒taḥ | bha॒dram | ma॒naḥ | kṛ॒ṇu॒ṣva | vṛ॒tra॒tūryḙ | ha॒viḥ | kṛ॒ṇu॒ṣva | su॒bha॒gaḥ | yathā̭ | a॒sa॒si॒ | bra॒hmaṇa̭ḥ | pa॒teḥ | arva̭: | ā | vṛ॒ṇī॒mahe॒ || 2 ||
ṛṣiḥ ātmīyaṃ puruṣaṃ śāsti |
he ᐞvīra viśeṣeṇa stutīnāṃ preraka sakhe ᐞyajasva brahmaṇaspatiṃ stutyā pūjaya |
ᐞmanāyataḥ mana iva śīghraṃ pravartamānān hiṃsakān |

vī gatyādiṣu |
chāndaso hrasvaḥ ||
ᐞvṛtratūrye vṛtrāṇāmāvārakāṇāṃ śatrūṇāṃ hiṃsāhetubhūte saṃgrāme ᐞbhadraṃ kalyāṇaṃ dhāṣṭryapi taṃ ᐞmanaḥ ᐞkṛṇuṣva kuruṣva |

sa i॒jjanai̭na sa vi॒śā sa ja̭nma॒nā sa pu॒trairvā॒jaṃ bha̭rate॒ dhanā̭ nṛ॒bhi: | de॒vānā̭ṃ yaḥ pi॒tara̭māvi॒vāsa̭ti śra॒ddhāma̭nā ha॒viṣā॒ brahma̭ṇa॒spati̭m || 3 || saḥ | it | ja॒nena̭ | saḥ | vi॒śā | saḥ | ja॒nma॒nā | saḥ | pu॒traiḥ | vā॒jam | bha॒ratḙ | dhanā̭ | nṛ॒bhiḥ | de॒vānā̭m | yaḥ | pi॒tara̭m | ā॒vi॒vā॒sa॒ti॒ | śra॒ddhā'ma̭nāḥ | ha॒viṣā̭ | bra॒hmaṇa̭ḥ | pa॒tim || 3 ||
ᐞyaḥ yajamānaḥ ᐞśraddhāmanāḥ śraddhā manasi yasya tādṛśaḥ san ᐞdevānāṃ ᐞpitaraṃ pālayitāraṃ ᐞbrahmaṇaspatiṃ ᐞhaviṣā carupuroḍāśādinā ᐞāvivāsati paricarati |

1. kha-ga-ca-ṇa-ṇa-ta-tha-na-va-bhajate; jha-ma-bhajata |

asmai brahmaṇaspataye |

'vitha vidhāne' |
naudādikaḥ |
ayaṃ paricaraṇakarmasu paṭhitaḥ ||
taṃ yajamānaṃ brahmaṇaspatiḥ prācā prācīnena ṛjunā mārgeṇa pra nayati prakṛṣṭaṃ phalaṃ prāpayati |
api ca īm enam aṃhasaḥ pāpān uruṣyati rakṣati |
'uruṣyatī rakṣākarmā' iti yāskaḥ |

aṃhościn anṛtadvarijñān |

5 ||
'imā giraḥ' iti saptadaśarcaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ gṛtsamadaputrasya kūrmasya āpaṃ gṛtsamadasyaiva vā traiṣṭubhādityadevatākam |
tathā cānukrāntam-'imā girastvasū karmā gārbhamado hi vādityam' iti |
sūktaviniyogo laiṅgikaḥ |
ādityadevatāke paśau puroḍāśabhya 'imā giraḥ' ityepā'nu- vākyā |
'agnīṣomau' iti khaṇḍe mūtritam-'imā gira ādityebhyo ghṛtastṛta ādityama uravo gabhīrāḥ' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
imā gira ādityebhyo ghṛtastṛḥ sunādrāja̭bhyo juhvā juhomi | śṛṇotu mitro aryamā bhago nastuvijāto varuṇo dakṣo aṃśaḥ || 1 || imāḥ | giraḥ | ādityebhyaḥ | ghṛta'stṛḥ | sunāt | rāja'bhyaḥ | juhvā | juhomi | śṛṇotu | mitraḥ | aryamā | bhagaḥ | naḥ | tuvijātaḥ | varuṇaḥ | dakṣaḥ | aṃśaḥ || 1 ||
ādityebhyaḥ aditaḥ putrebhyaḥ rājabhyaḥ rājamānebhya īśvarebhya vā mitrādibhyaḥ ghṛtastṛḥ ghṛtaprastrāviṇīghṛtaṃ kṣarantīḥ imā giraḥ stutilakṣaṇā vācaḥ sanāt sarvadā juhvā |
vāṅnāmaiṭat |
vāgindriyeṇa juhomi prayacchāmi2 karomīti yāvat |
yadvā juhrasthānīyena ghṛtaṃ kṣaranti vāgrūpāṇi havīṃṣi juhomi ādityānudiśya tyajāmi |
mantrāṇāṃ payogatādikṣaraṇahetutvaṃ ca 'yacco'dhīte payasaḥ kṛtyā asya' (tai. ā. 2. 10) ityādinā taittirīye spaṣṭamnātam |
naḥ asmadīyāstā giro mitrādayaḥ pratyekaṃ śṛṇotu |
te ca taittirīye 'aṣṭau putrāso aditaḥ' ityupakramya spaṣṭamanukrāntāḥ- 'mitraśca varuṇa dhātā cārdhamā ca aṃśuśca bhagaśca indraśca vivasvāṃścaiyete' (tai. ā. 1. 13. 3) iti |
mitraḥ pramītestrāyakaḥ |
yadvā sarveṣāṃ snigdhaḥ |
arīna yacchati niyacchatīti aryamā |
bhagaḥ bhajanīyo devaḥ |
tuvīti bahunāma |
bahuṣu deśeṣu anugrahārthaṃ prādurbhūtaḥ |
varuṇaviśeṣaṇametat |
pāpasya nivāraṇāt varuṇaḥ |
dakṣaḥ samarthaḥ |
etacca aṃśasya viśeṣaṇam |
ete pratyekaṃ śṛṇvantu ||
pūrvokta eva paśau 'imaṃ stomam' ityeṣā vapāyā6 yājyā6 |
sūtritaṃ ca-'imaṃ stomaṃ sakratavo me adya tinno bhūmīrdhārayan trīṃrata dhūna' (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||

haṃ ᵥvamavaḥ vāsavināra ādityāḥ ᵥyuṣmānītaḥ yuṣmābhirnītaḥ śobhanena mārgeṇa prāptaḥ san ᵥabhayaṃ bhayarahitaṃ ᵥjyotiḥ sauraṃ tejaḥ āntaraṃ vā jñānātmakam ᵥaśyāṃ prāpnuyām ||
yo rāja̭bhya ṛ॒tuni̭bhyo॒ dadā̭śa॒ yaṃ va̭rdhayanti pu॒ṣṭya̭śca nityāḥ | sa re॒vānyā̭ti॒ pratha॒mo rathḙna va॒sudā̭vā॒ vida̭theṣu॒ praśa̭staḥ || 12 || yaḥ | rāja̭bhyaḥ | ṛ॒tuni̭bhyaḥ | dadā̭śa | yam | va̭rdhayanti | pu॒ṣṭya̭ḥ | ca | nityāḥ | saḥ | re॒vān | yā̭ti | pratha॒maḥ | rathḙna | va॒sudā̭vā | vida̭theṣu | pra'śa̭staḥ || 12 ||
ᵥyaḥ: yajamānaḥ ᵥrājabhyaḥ rājamānebhyaḥ ᵥṛtanibhyaḥ ṛtasya yajñasya² satyasya vā netṛbhya ādityebhyaḥ ᵥdadāśa carupuroḍāśādikaṃ dadāti |

ᵥsaḥ yajamānaḥ ᵥrevān dhanavān ᵥprathamaḥ prathitaḥ prakhyātaḥ ᵥvasudāvā vasūnāṃ dhanānāṃ dātā san ᵥpraśastaḥ sarvaiḥ praśasyamānaḥ ᵥvidadhepu vedhepu labdhavyeṣu gṛheṣu ᵥrathena vāhanena ᵥyāti gacchati ||
śuci̭rapaḥ suyava̭sā a॒dabdha u॒pa kṣeti̭ vṛ॒ddharva̭yāḥ suvī̭raḥ | naki̭ṣṭaṃ ma॒ntyanti̭to na du॒rādya ā̭di॒tyānā̭ṃ bha॒vati̭ praṇī॒tau || 13 || śuci̭ḥ | apa̭ḥ | su'yava̭sā | a॒dabdhaḥ | upa̭ | kṣeti̭ | vṛ॒ddha'va̭yāḥ | su'vī̭raḥ | naki̭ḥ | tam | ghnanti̭ | anti̭taḥ | na | du॒rāt | yaḥ | ā̭di॒tyānā̭m | bha॒vati̭ | pra'nī̭tau || 13 ||

idam | kaveḥ | ādityasya | sva'rājaḥ | viśvāni | santi | abhi | astu | mahnā | ati | yaḥ | mandraḥ | yajathāya | devaḥ | su'kīrtim | bhikṣe | varuṇasya | bhūraiḥ || 1 ||
^v kaveḥ krāntadarśinaḥ ^v ādityasya aditaḥ putrasya ^v svarājaḥ svayameva rājamānasya īśvarasya vā ^v varuṇasya ^v idaṃ haviḥ stotraṃ vā yajamānaḥ karotīti śeṣaḥ |

1. ka-ta-tha-na-ma-va- ' tathā ... trāyadhvam ' nāsti |

1 ||
ā | tḙ | pi॒taḥ | ma॒rutā̭m | sunma̭m | e॒tu | mā | na॒ḥ | sūrya̭sya | sam'dṛśa̭ḥ | yu॒yothā̭ḥ | a॒bhi | na॒ḥ | vī॒raḥ | a॒rva॒ti | kṣḙmat | pra | jā॒ye॒ma॒hi॒ | rudra̭ | pra॒'jābhi̭ḥ || 1 ||

haṃ murutaḥ ᵥevayānḥ evaṃ gantavyaṃ stotraṃ yajñaṃ vā gantṛn ᵥmahaḥ mahato mahānubhāvān ᵥtān ᵥvaḥ yuṣmān ᵥviṣṇoḥ vyāpakasya mahacamasādiṣu sarvatra vyāpya vartamānasya ᵥeṣasya eṣaṇīyasya prārthanīyasya somasya ᵥprabhṛthe prabharaṇe abhipravādibhiḥ prakarṣeṇa saṃpādane sati ᵥhavāmahe āhvayāmahe |

^tān marutaḥ ^mahi mahat ^varūthaṃ varaṇīyaṃ praśastaṃ dhanaṃ tat marutaḥ ^ūtaye rakṣaṇārtham ^īyāna: īyānā yācamānā vayam ||

1. kha-ga-ṇa-ta-tha-ma-viśvā |
apolaptrīye vasatīvarīsamīpe ekadhanāsu' prāptāsu ' samanyā yanti ' ityepā'nuvaktavyā |

‘ ^tubhyam ^’ ^iti ^ṣaṭkaṃ ^caturthaṃ ^sūktaṃ ^gātsamadaṃ ^jāgatam ^|

^tathā ^cānukrāntaṃ ^— ^‘ ^tubhyaṃ ^ṣaṭkavyaṃ ^tu ^jāgataṃ ^tu ^’ ^iti ^|
^daśarātre paṣṭhe'hani ^ṛtuyājeṣu ^ṛkśiraskaiḥ ^prepaiḥ ^prepitavyaṃ ^yaṣṭavyaṃ ^ca ^|

^tathā ^ca ^sūtritam ^— ^‘ ^upariṣṭhācca ^ṛtuyājānām ^’ ^ityupakramya ‘ ^tubhyaṃ ^hinvāno ^vamiṣṭa ^gā ^apa ^iti ^dvādaśa ^’ ^( ^āśva. ^śrau. ^8.1 ^) ^ityantena ^||

1 ||
tubhyam | ^hinvānaḥ | ^vasiṣṭa ^| ^gāḥ | ^apaḥ | ^adhukṣan | ^sīma | ^avirbhiḥ | ^adribhiḥ | ^naraḥ | piba | ^indra | ^svāhā | ^pra'hutam | ^varṣatkṛtam | ^| hotrāt | ^ā | ^somam | ^prathamaḥ | ^yaḥ | ^īśiṣe || 1 ||
he ^indra ^tubhyaṃ ^tvadartha ^hinvānaḥ ^preryamāṇaḥ ^somaḥ ^gāḥ ^govikārāṇi ^dadhipayaḥprabhṛtīni śrayaṇadravyāṇi ^vasatīvaryākhyāḥ ^apaḥ ^ca ^vasiṣṭa ^vaste ^ācchādayati ^|

1. ^ca-tha- ^° ^jananātharma ^| ^2. ^kha-ca-pha- ^uccārayāma ^| ^u ^pūraṇaḥ ^; ^ga-ṇa1- ^uccārayāma ^| ^pūraṇaḥ ^| ka. ^2-20
jagata īśvaro bhavasi saḥ 1 tvaṃ prathamaḥ anyebhyo devebhyaḥ pūrvabhāvī san svāhā svāhākāreṇa prahutaṃ prakarṣeṇāgnau kṣisaṃ vapaṭkṛtaṃ vapaṭkāreṇa ca vyaktaṃ taṃ somaṃ hotrāt hotaryāgāta ā samantāt piba ||
yajñaiḥ saṃmiślāḥ pṛṣatībhirṛṣṭibhiryāmanśubhrāso agniṣu priyā uta | āsadyā barhirbharatasya sūnavaḥ potrādā somaṃ pibatā divo naraḥ || 2 || yajñaiḥ | saṃ'miślāḥ | pṛṣatībhiḥ | ṛṣṭi'bhiḥ | yāman | śubhrāsaḥ | agniṣu | priyāḥ | uta | ā'sadya | barhiḥ | bharatasya | sūnavaḥ | potrāt | ā | somam | pibata | divaḥ | naraḥ ||2||
he marutaḥ yajñaiḥ yāgaiḥ saṃmiślāḥ saṃyuktāḥ pṛṣatībhiḥ svavāhanabhūtābhiḥ pṛṣadbhīrbhirvarvābhiryukte yāman yāmani gamanasādhane rathe'vasthitāḥ ṛṣṭibhiḥ svakīyairāyudhaiḥ śubhrāsaḥ śubhrāḥ śobhamānāḥ |
utaśabdaścārthe |
agniṣu ābharaṇeṣu priyāḥ ca he bharatasya sūnavaḥ sarvasya jagato bhartu rudrasya putrāḥ divo naraḥ divo'ntarikṣasya netāraḥ evaṃbhūtā he marutaste yūyaṃ barhiḥ āstīrṇe barhiṣi āsadya upaviśya potrāt potryāgāta somam ā pibata ||
tṛtīyasavane 'ameva naḥ' ityepā neṣṭuḥ sthitayājyā ca |
tathā ca sūtritam—'ameva naḥ suhavā ā hi gantanendrāviṣṇū pibataṃ madhvo asya' (āśva. śrau. 5. 5 ) iti ||
amevaṃ naḥ suhavā ā hi gantaṃ ni barhiṣi sadata raṇiṣṭana | athā mandasva jujuṣāṇo andhasastvaṣṭevebhirjanibhiḥ sumadraṇaḥ || 3 || amā'īva | naḥ | su'havāḥ | ā | hi | gantan | ni | barhiṣi | sadata | raṇiṣṭana | atha | mandasva | jujuṣāṇaḥ | andhasaḥ | tvaṣṭaḥ | devebhiḥ | janibhiḥ | sumata'gaṇaḥ ||3||
ivaśabdo'vadhāraṇe |
he suhavāḥ śobhanāhvānāstvaṣṭaprabhṛtayaḥ naḥ asmān ameva sahaiva ā gantana āgacchata |
gameśchāndasaḥ śapo luk |
'tasanasanathanātha' iti tanabādeśaḥ |
hiśabdaḥ pūraṇaḥ ||
āgasya ca barhiṣi vedyāmāstīrṇe ni sadatana nipaṇṇā bhavata ||
sadeśchāndasaḥ sīdādeśābhāvaḥ ||
upaviśya ca raṇiṣṭana ramadhvam ||
raṇa śabdārthaḥ |
atra krīḍāyāṃ vartate dhātunāmanekārthatvāt |
asmā- cchāndaso luk |
pūrvavattanabādeśaḥ ||
atha anantaraṃ he tvaṣṭaḥ devebhiḥ anyairdevaiḥ janibhiḥ devapatnībhiśca sumadraṇaḥ śobhanagaṇastvam andhasaḥ somalakṣaṇasyānnasya |
dvitīyārthe ṣaṣṭhī |
somalakṣaṇamannaṃ jujuṣāṇaḥ sevamānaḥ mandasva tṛpto bhava ||
ā vāṃkṣi devā~ iha vipra yakṣi co'śanhautārni ṣadā yoniṣu triṣu | prati vīhi prasthitaṃ somyaṃ madhu pibāmrīdhāttvaṃ bhāgasya tṛpṇuhi || 4 || ā | vakṣi | devān | iha | vipra | yakṣi | ca | uśan | hotaḥ | ni | sada | yoniṣu | triṣu | prati | vīhi | pra'sthitam | somyam | madhu | piba | āgnīdhrāt | tava | bhāgasya | tṛpṇuhi ||4||
1. kha-ga-ca-jha-ṇa-ṇa-tha-na-va-'saḥ' nāsti |

he mitrāvaruṇau madīyamimaṃ <sup>v</sup>yajñaṃ <sup>v</sup>juṣethāṃ sevethām |

vāṅnāmaiṭatat |

tādṛśaṃ <sup>v</sup>somyaṃ somamayaṃ <sup>v</sup>madhu mādakaṃ haviḥ <sup>v</sup>praśāstrāt praśāstṛryāgāt <sup>v</sup>ā <sup>v</sup>pibatam ābhimukhyena pibatam |

vedārthasya prakāśena tamo hādvaṃ nivārayan |
pumarthāṃścaturo deyāddyātīrthamaheśvaraḥ |
iti śrīmadrājādhirājaparameśvaravaidikamārgapravartakaśrīvīrabukabhūpālasāmrājyadhuraṃdhareṇa sāyaṇācāryeṇa viracite mādhavīye vedārthaprakāśe ṛksaṃhitābhāṣye dvitīyāṣṭake saptamo'dhyāyaḥ samāptaḥ |
1. kha-ga-ṇa-ta-tha-ma-niṣoda |
2. ka-jña-ma-āmīdhrāgāt; kha-ga-ṇa-na-va-abhīdho yāgāt |
3. ka-ma-sāyaṇāmātyena; ta-sāyaṇāyena |
śrīgaṇeśāya namaḥ ||
yasya niḥśvasitaṃ vedā yo vedebhyo'khilaṃ jagat |
nirmame tamahaṃ vande vidyātīrthamaheśvaram ||
atha dvitīyāṣṭakasyāṣṭamo'dhyāya ārabhyate |
dvitīyamaṇḍalasya caturthānuvāke ‘mandasva’ iti ṣaṭkaṃ pañcamaṃ sūktaṃ gārsamadam |
‘mandasva’ ityanukrāntam |
pūrvetra ‘ṛtavyaṃ tu jāgataṃ tu’ iti tuśabda- prayogādidamapi jāgatamṛtavyam |
tatrādyāstasra ṛco draviṇodevatākāḥ pañcamyāśvinī ṣaṣṭhyāmevī |
daśarātre ṣaṣṭhe'hani ṛtuyājeṣu praipānatṛśiraskān kṛtvā taiḥ praipaiḥ preṣitavyaṃ yaṣṭavyaṃ ca |
tatra saptamādīnāṃ praipāṇāmupariṣṭāt ‘mandasva’ ityādyāḥ ṣaṭkaḥ krameṇa prakṣepaṇīyāḥ |
sūtraṃ tu pūrvamevodāhṛtam ||
mandasva hotrādanu joṣamandhaso'dhvaryavaḥ sa pūrṇā vaṣṭyāsicam | tasmāṃ etaṃ bharata tadvaśo dadirhotrātsomaṃ draviṇodaḥ piba ṛtubhiḥ || 1 || mandasva | hotrāt | anu | jo
u iti pūraṇe |
va dradiḥ abhīṣṭaphalasya dātā vayo vanāma yaḥ khalu vapaśyate devatātveneṣṭa tarasmai draviṇodase vasomyaṃ somātmakaṃ vamadhu vaadhvaryubhiḥ vaprasthitaṃ praṇītamāsīt |
haṃ vadraviṇodaḥ vapotrāt poturyāgāna vaṛtubhiḥ urjasaṃjñakaiḥ saha vasomaṃ vapiba ||
medyantu te vahvayo yebhirīyase'riṣaṇyanvīḻayasva vanaspate | āyuyā dhṛṣṇo abhigūryā tvaṃ neṣṭātsomaṃ draviṇodaḥ piba ṛtubhiḥ || 3 || medyantu | te | vahvayaḥ | yebhiḥ | īyase | ariṣaṇyan | vīḻayasva | vanaspate | āyuyā | dhṛṣṇo iti | abhigūrya | tvam | neṣṭāt | somam | draviṇaḥ'daḥ | piba | ṛtubhiḥ || 3 ||
he vadraviṇodaḥ vayebhiḥ yairvāheḥ vaīyase prāpnopi |
vate tava ‘vahvayo voḍhāraḥ’ (niru. 8. 3) iti yāskenoktatvāt |
te vamedyantu tṛpyantu |
he vavanaspate vanānāṃ pate vaariṣaṇyan ahiṃsan vavīḻayasva dṛḍhībhava |
he vadhṛṣṇo dharṣaka vatvam vaāyuya āgamya vaabhigūrya udyamya vaneṣṭāt neṣṭaryāṃgāt vaṛtubhiḥ sahaḥsaṃjñakaiḥ saha vasomaṃ vapiba ||
apāddhotrāduta potrādamata neṣṭādajuṣata praryo hitam | turīyaṃ pātramṛktumamartyaṃ draviṇodāḥ pibatu drāviṇodasaḥ || 4 || apāt | hotrāt | uta | potrāt | amatta | uta | neṣṭāt | ajuṣata | praryaḥ | hitam | turīyam | pātram | amṛktam | amartyam | draviṇaḥ'dāḥ | pibatu | drāviṇaḥ'dasaḥ || 4 ||
acchāvākasyeyam |
yo draviṇodāḥ ‘hotrāt hotaryāṃgāt somam vaapāt pītavān |
vauta kiṃca vapotrāt poturyāṃgāt vaamatta somaṃ pītvā amādyat |
vauta api ca vaneṣṭāt neṣṭaryāṃgāt vahitaṃ dattaṃ vaprayaḥ devānānnarūpaṃ somam vaajuṣata aseviṣṭa |
saḥ vadrāviṇodasaḥ havīrūpadraviṇasya pradātṛbhya ṛtvigbhyo hitaḥ ||
‘ṛtvijo'tra draviṇodasa ucyante haviṣo dātāraste caimaṃ janayanti’ (niru. 8. 2) iti yāskenoktatvāt ||
tādṛśaḥ vadraviṇodāḥ vaamṛktaṃ daśāpavitreṇa amṛṣṭam vaamartyam amarasādhanaṃ vaturīyaṃ vapātraṃ caturthapātrasthaṃ somaṃ vapibatu ||
arvāścamadya yuyyaṃ nuvāhaṃ rathe yuñjāthāmiha vāṃ vimocanam | puḍūṃ havīṃṣi madhunā hi kaṃ gṛthamathā somaṃ pibataṃ vājinīvasū || 5 || arvāścam | adya | yuyyam | nu'vāham | ratham | yuñjāthām | iha | vām | vi'mocanam | puḍam | havīṃṣi | madhunā | ā | hi | kam | gṛtham | atha | somam | pibatam | vājinīvasū iti vājinī'vasū || 5 ||
1. kha-ṇa-dha-ma-janayanti ca |
he aśvinau ᵛadya asminnahani ᵛyadyaṃ yathi gantāraṃ ᵛnṛvāhanaṃ netroryavayovoḍhāram ᵛiha yajñe ᵛvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵛvimocanaṃ vimocayitāraṃ ᵛratham ᵛarvāñcaṃ yathā arvāk bhavati tathā ᵛyuṣmāthām |
kiṃca ᵛmadhunā svādurasena ᵛhavīṃṣi ᵛpṛṅktaṃ saṃyojayatam |
atha ca ᵛā ᵛgatam āgacchatam ||
ᵛhi ᵛkam iti pūraṇe ||
ᵛatha anantaraṃ he ᵛvājinīvasū |
vāja eva vājinī |
annena vāsayitārau balavardhanau vā ᵛsomaṃ ᵛpivatam ||
joṣya̭gne ॒samidha॒ṃ joṣyāhṷtiṃ joṣi॒ brahma̭ janyaṃ joṣi̭ su॒ṣṭuti̭m | viśvḙbhi॒rviśvā̭ ṛ॒tūnā॒ṃ vaso॒ maha॒ uśa̭nde॒vā~ ṷśataḥ pā॒yayā̭ ha॒viḥ || 6 || joṣi̭ | agne | sa॒midha̭m | joṣi̭ | ā'hṷtim | joṣi̭ | brahma̭ | janya̭m | joṣi̭ | su॒ṣṭuti̭m | viśvḙbhiḥ | viśvā̭n | ṛ॒tūnā̭m | vaso॒ iti̭ | mahaḥ | u॒śan | de॒vān | u॒śata̭ḥ | pā॒yaya̭ | ha॒viḥ || 6 ||
he ᵛagne ᵛsamidhaṃ ᵛjoṣi jupasva |
ᵛāhutiṃ ᵛjoṣi jupasva |

‘ grāvāṇeva ’ ityṛcaṃ saptamaṃ sūktaṃ gārtsamadaṃ traiṣṭubhamāśvinaṃ ‘ grāvāṇevāśvāśvinam ’ ityanu- krāntatvāt |

1. ṇa-tha-na-va-ukthaśasā |
he aśvinau aśveva paśoḥ śraddhe iva prathamā devānāṃ prathamau śaphāviva aśvādeḥ khurāviva tarobhiḥ vegaiḥ jarbhurāṇā bhṛśaṃ gacchantau yuvāṃ naḥ asmānprati arvāk abhimukham ā gantam āgacchatam |
kiṃca he aśvinau vastoḥ prati ahaḥ prati cakravākeva cakravākāviva he ustrā śatrūṇāmutsārakau gamanaśīlau vā ratheva rathinaviva he śakrā svakarmaṇi śaktau arvāñcā asmada- bhimukhau yāta gacchatam ||
nāveva naḥ pārayataṃ yugeva nabhyaiva na upadhīva pradhīva |
śvāneva no aripaṇyā tanūnāṃ khagaleva visrasaḥ pātamusmān ||
4 ||
nāvā'iva | naḥ | pārayatam | yugā'iva | nabhyā'iva | naḥ | upadhī ivetyupadhī'iva | pradhī iveti pradhī'iva | śvānā'iva | naḥ | aripaṇyā | tanūnām | khagalā'iva | visrasaḥ | pātam | asmān ||4||
he aśvinau nāveva nāvāviva sindhūna naḥ asmān durgāṇi pārayatam |
yugeva yathā rathasya yuge nabhyaiva yathā ca rathacakranābhiphalake |
upadhīhava yathā tatpārśvasthe phalake pradhīhava yathā vā cakrabāhavalayī rathaṃ pārayataḥ tathā naḥ asmān durgāṇi pārayatam |
kiṃca śvāneva śvānāviva yathā śvānau bādhakebhyo rakṣantau tanorhiṃsāmapanayataḥ tathā naḥ asmākaṃ tanūnām aṅgānām aripaṇyau ahiṃsakau bhavatam |
khagaleva yathā tanutrāṇe tanuhiṃsāyā rakṣataḥ tathā visrasaḥ jarāyāḥ asmān yuvāṃ pātam ||
vātevājuryā nṛyaiva ritarakṣīva cakṣuṣā yātamurvāk |

vātā'iva | ajuryā | nṛyā'iva | ritiḥ | akṣī ivetyakṣī iva | cakṣuṣā | ā | yātam | arvāk | hastā'iva | tanve | śam'aviṣṭhā | pādā'iva | naḥ | nayatam | vasyaḥ | accha || 5 ||
he aśvinau vāteva vātāviva ajuryā jaritumaśakyau naceva nadyāviva ritiḥ vyatyaye- naikavacanam ||
śīghraṃ gantārau yuvām |
akṣīhava akṣiṇī iva cakṣuṣā darśanena arvāk asmadabhimukham ā yātam āgacchatam |

oṣṭhau'iva | madhu | āste | vadantā | stanau'iva | pipyatam | jīvaseṃ | naḥ | nāsā'iva | naḥ | tanvau | rakṣitārā | karṇau'iva | suśrutā | bhūtam | asme iti || 6 ||
he aśvinau ᵛoṣṭhāviva ᵛāsne āsthāya madhurarasavahvacanaṃ ᵛstanāvica ᵛnaḥ asmān ᵛjīvase jīvanāya ᵛvipyataṃ pyāyayatm |
kiṃ ca ᵛnāseva nāsike iva ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛtanvaḥ tanoḥ ᵛrakṣitārā rakṣitārau bhūtaṃ bhavatam |
api ca ᵛkarṇāviva ᵛasme asmākaṃ ᵛsuśrutā suśravaṇe yuvāṃ ᵛbhūtaṃ bhavatam ||
hastaiva śaktimābhi saṃdadī naḥ kṣāmeva naḥ sarmajataṃ rajāṃsi |
imā giroṃ aśvinā yuṣmayantīḥ kṣaṇatreṇaiva svadhitiṃ saṃ śiśītam ||

hastā'iva | śaktim | abhi | saṃdadī iti suma'dadī | naḥ | kṣāma'iva | naḥ | sam | ajatam | rajāṃsi | imāḥ | giraḥ | aśvinā | yuṣmayantīḥ | kṣaṇatreṇa'iva | svadhitim | sam | śiśītam ||7||
he aśvinau ᵛhasteva hastāviva ᵛśakti sāmarthyaṃ ᵛnaḥ asmākam ᵛabhi ᵛsaṃdadī abhimumyena samyak prayacchantau bhavatam |
kiṃ ca ᵛkṣāmeva rodamī iva ᵛrajāmi sthānānyudakāni vā |
‘udakaṃ raja ucyate’ (niru. 4. 19) iti yāskaḥ |
ᵛnaḥ asmākaṃ ᵛsamajataṃ prerayatm |
api ca ᵛaśvinā he aśvinau ᵛimā ᵛgiraḥ asmābhiḥ kṛtāḥ stutīḥ ᵛyuṣmayantīḥ yuvāmicchantīḥ ᵛsvadhitim asiṃ ᵛkṣaṇatreṇaiva tejanaśāṇavat ᵛsaṃ ᵛśiśītaṃ samyak tīkṣṇīkurutam ||
etāni vāmaśvinā vardhanāni brahma stomaṃ gṛtsamadāsau akran |
tāni narā jujuṣāṇopa yātaṃ bṛhaddema vidatheṃ suvīrāṃḥ ||

ᵛaśvinā he aśvinau ᵛvāṃ yuvayoḥ ᵛetāni ᵛvardhanāni vṛddhisādhanāni ᵛbrahma brahmāṇi mantrān ᵛstomaṃ stotraṃ ca ᵛgṛtsamadāsaḥ gṛtsamadāḥ ᵛakran akurvan |
ᵛtāni brahmāṇi ᵛnarā netārī ᵛjujuṣāṇā bhṛśaṃ prīyamāṇau yuvām ᵛupa ᵛyātam upagacchatam |
kiṃ ca ᵛsuvīrāḥ śobhanaputrā vayaṃ ᵛvidathe yajñe yuvāṃ ᵛbṛhaddema prabhūtaṃ stumaḥ ||
5 ||
‘somāpūṣaṇā’ iti ṣaṭkamaṣṭamaṃ sūktaṃ gārhasamadaṃ traiṣṭubhaṃ somāpūṣadevatākam |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ- ‘somāpūṣaṇā ṣaṭ somāpūṣṇamanyordharcopyaditeḥ’ iti |
‘avatu dedhyaditiḥ’ ityasya ᵛananyā- dharcasya aditirdevatā |
somāpūṣṇe paśau vapāpuroḍāśahaviṣāmādyāstisraḥ krameṇānuvākyā uttarastisro yājyāḥ |
‘agnīṣomau’ iti khaṇḍe sūtritaṃ—‘somāpūṣaṇā jananā rayīṇām’ (āśva. śrau. 3. 8) iti ||
1. ka-jha-jha 1-ṇa-ṇa 1-tha-na-ma-va-madhurasa0 |
2. ma-ityetasya |
somāpūṣaṇā jananā rayīṇāṃ jananā divo jananā pṛthivyāḥ | jātau viśvasya bhuvanasya gopau devā akṛṇvannamṛtasya nābhim || 1 || somāpūṣaṇā | jananā | rayīṇām | jananā | divaḥ | jananā | pṛthivyāḥ | jātau | viśvasya | bhuvanasya | gopau | devāḥ | akṛṇvan | amṛtasya | nābhim || 1 ||
vsomāpūṣaṇā he somāpūṣaṇau yuvāṃ vrayīṇāṃ dhanānāṃ vjananā janayitārau vdivaḥ dyulokasyāpi vjananā janayitārau vpṛthivyāḥ pṛthivyāśca janayitārau bhavataḥ |
kiṃca vjātau jātamātrāveva yuvāṃ vviśvasya vbhuvanasya kṛtsnasya jagataḥ vgopau goptārau vdevāḥ vamṛtasya amarasya vnābhiṃ hetum vakṛṇvan akārpuḥ ||
imau devau jāyamānau juṣantemau tamāṃsi gṛhatāmajuṣṭā | ābhyāmindraḥ pakmāmāsvantaḥ somāpuṣbhyāṃ janadustriyāsu || 2 || imau | devau | jāyamānau | juṣanta | imau | tamāṃsi | gṛhatām | ajuṣṭā | ābhyām | indraḥ | pakmam | āmāsu | antariti | somāpūṣabhyām | janat | usriyāsu ||2||
vimau somāpūṣaṇau vdevau dyotamānau vjāyamānau utpannamātrāveva vjuṣanta sarve devā ajupanta |
sevitavanta iti yāvata |
kiṃca vimau somāpūṣaṇau vajuṣṭā asevyāni vtamāṃsi sarvasya vgṛhatāṃ nāśayata: |
kiṃca vābhyām etābhyāṃ vsomāpūṣabhyāṃ saha vindraḥ vāmāsu taraṇīṣu vusriyāsu goṣu vantaḥ madhye ūdhaḥpradeśe vpakaṃ paripakaṃ payaḥ vjanat janayati ||
somāpūṣaṇā rajaso vimānaṃ saptacakraṃ rathamiśvāminvam | viṣpavṛttaṃ manasā yujyamānaṃ taṃ jinvatho vṛṣaṇā pañcaraśmim || 3 || somāpūṣaṇā | rajasaḥ | vi'mānam | sapta'cakram | ratham | aviśva'minvam | viṣ'vṛttam | manasā | yujyamānam | tam | jinvathaḥ | vṛṣaṇā | pañca'raśmim || 3 ||
vvṛṣaṇā vṛṣaṇau kāmānāṃ varṣitārau vsomāpūṣaṇā he somāpūṣaṇau vrajasaḥ lokasya |
‘lokā rajāṃsyu- cyante’ (niru.4.19) iti yāskaḥ |
vvimānaṃ paricchedakam |
sarvamānam’ ityarthaḥ |
vsaptacakraṃ saptacakram |

vaviśvaminvaṃ viśvasyāparicchedyaṃ vviṣpavṛttaṃ vipvaka sarvatra parivartamānaṃ vpañcaraśmiṃ pañcaraśmim |
hemantaśiśirayoḥ
1, ka-jha-sarvavam; jha1-sarvabhūtam; ṇa-ta-tha-na-va-sarvamatam; ma-sarvatam |
samāsena ṛtavaḥ pañca |
tathā ca śrūyate—‘dvādaśa māsāḥ pañcartavo hemantaśiśirayoḥ samāsena’ (ai. brā. 1. 1) iti |
manasā yujyamānaṃ manomātreṇa yujyamānam |
taṃ prasiddhaṃ rathaṃ jinnathaḥ asmānprati prerayathaḥ ||
divyanyaḥ sadanaṃ cakra uccā pṛthivyāmanyo adhyantarikṣe |

4 ||
divi | anyaḥ | sadanam | cakre | uccā | pṛthivyām | anyaḥ | adhi | antarikṣe | tau | asmabhyam | puruvāram | purukṣum | rāyaḥ | poṣam | vi | syatām | nābhim | asme iti || 4 ||
anyaḥ tayorekaḥ pūṣā uccā uccairucchrite divi dhuloke sadanaṃ sthānaṃ cakre cakāra |
anyaḥ aparaḥ somaḥ pṛthivyām oṣadhirūpeṇa adhyantarikṣe antarikṣe ca candrarūpeṇa sadanaṃ cakre |
tau somāpūṣaṇau yuvāṃ puruvāraṃ bahubhirvarṇīyaṃ purukṣuṃ bahukīrtiṃ rāyaspoṣaṃ dhanasya puṣṭim |
paścātmakaṃ dhanamityarthaḥ |

5 ||

tau yuvāṃ somāpūṣaṇau me dhiyaṃ karme |

avataṃ rakṣatam |

‘vāyo ye te’ ityekaviṃśatyṛcaṃ navamaṃ sūktaṃ gāsramadaṃ gāyatram |
‘ambitame’ iti ṣaṣṭhasya tṛcasya ādito dve anuṣṭubhau tṛtīyā bṛhatī |
‘vāyo ye te’ ityādi ke dve ṛcau vāyavyau |
‘śukrasyādha’ ityeṣā aindravāyavī |
‘ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā’ iti pañcānāṃ tṛcānāṃ mitrāvaruṇau aśvinau indraḥ viśve devāḥ sarasvatī iti krameṇa devatāḥ |
‘pretāṃ yajñasya’ ityasyamṛco brāhmaṇasūtrayorvidhānapravartane dyāvāpṛthivyanividvānatvena ca viniyuktatvāt dyāvāpṛthivyo havirdhānadevatyo vā |
‘agniṃ ca havya- vāhanam’ iti pādo'pridevatyo vā |
tathā cānukrāntaṃ- ‘vāyo saikā gāyatramuktā devatāḥ praugeṇādhe tu tṛce'nyaindravāyavī dyāvāpṛthivyo'nyastṛco hāvirdhāno vā tṛtīyaḥ pādo vāyavyo'mbitame'nuṣṭubhau bṛhatī ca’ iti |
pṛṣṭhābhiplavapaḍahayordvitīyeḍahani prauge'nyatṛcavarjametatsūktam |
vāyavyatṛcasya ‘vāyo ye te sahasriṇaḥ’ ityādye dve ṛcau aindravāyavatṛcasya ‘śukrasyādha’ ityeṣā ca sūtritaṃ- ‘vāyo ye te sahasriṇa iti tīvrāḥ somāsa ā gahītyekobhā devā divispṛśeti dve śukrasyādha gavāśira ityekāyaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇeti pañca tṛcāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 6) iti |
cāturviṃśike'hani prātaḥ- savane maitrāvaruṇaśastre ‘ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā’ iti paḻahastonitrasaṃjñakaḥ |
‘caturviṃśe hotājaniṣṭa’ iti khaṇḍe sūtritam- ‘ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā pururuṇā cidvayasti’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 2) iti |
ābhiplavike'haḥsu prātaḥsavane maitrāvaruṇaśastre'yameva tṛcaḥ pañcadaśādiṣṭomanamittamāvāpanīyaḥ |
‘ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā no mitrāvaruṇeti tṛcāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 5) iti sūtritatvāt |
ādyā maitrāvaruṇaprahasyānuvākyā |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘maitrāvaruṇasya ayaṃ vāṃ mitrāvaruṇā’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 5) iti |
prātaranuvākāśvinaśastrayogyāntre chandasi ‘gomada pu’ iti tṛco viniyuktaḥ |
‘gomad pu nāsyeti tṛcāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 15) iti sūtritatvāt |
atirātre prathame paryāye brāhmaṇācchaṃsi- śastre ‘indro aṅga mahaddhayam’ iti tṛcaḥ |
‘atirātre paryāyāṇām’ iti khaṇḍe sūtritam- ‘indro aṅga mahaddhayamabhi nyū pu vācam’ (āśva. śrau. 6.4) iti |
āgrayaṇe vaiśvadevasya havipaḥ ‘viśve devāsaḥ’ ityanuvākyā |

daśarātre prathame'hani praugaśastre ‘ambitame nadītame’ iti sārasvataḥ saptamastṛcaḥ |
‘caturthe'hani’ iti khaṇḍe sūtritam- ‘ambitame nadītama ityānuṣṭubhaṃ praugam’ (āśva. śrau. 7. 11) iti |
vyūḍhe daśarātre prathame chandome vaiśvadevaśastre ‘pretāṃ yajñasya śaṃbhuvā’ iti dyāvāpṛthivyanividvānīyatṛcaḥ |
‘atha chandomāḥ’ iti khaṇḍe sūtritaṃ- ‘pretāṃ yajñasya śaṃbhuvāyaṃ devāya janman iti tṛcāḥ’ (āśva. śrau. 8. 9) iti |
havirdhānapravartane'pyayaṃ tṛco viniyuktaḥ |
sūtritaṃ ca- ‘pretāṃ yajñasya śaṃbhuvā yame hava yatamāne yadaitam’ (āśva. śrau. 4. 9; ai. brā. 1. 29) iti ||

pradakṣiṇidbhi gṛṇanti kāravo vayo vadanta ṛtuthā śakunteyaḥ | ubhe vācau vadati sāmagāiva gāyatraṃ ca traiṣṭubhaṃ cānu rājati || 1 || pra'dakṣiṇit | abhi | gṛṇanti | kāravaḥ | vayaḥ | vadantaḥ | ṛtu'thā | śakunteyaḥ | ubhe iti | vācau | vadati | sāmagā'iva | gāyatram | ca | traiṣṭubham | ca | anu | rājati ||1||
śakunteyaḥ kapiñjalāḥ |
